- **Hypothesis** - the seal marker is sound iff it flips to SEALED in the SAME transaction as the final entity/fingerprint commit (H330's design), giving SEALED ==> all writes durable and OPEN ==> at least one write missing under every crash point; a marker written in a SEPARATE transaction from the final commit admits a window where SEALED lies
- **Prediction** - same-transaction seal -> 0 false-SEALED and 0 false-OPEN across injected crash points (before-first-write, mid-wave, after-last-write-before-seal, after-seal); separate-transaction seal -> >=1 false-SEALED at the after-commit-before-seal crash point, which would silently corrupt H330's torn-write detection and H335's quiescence decision
- **Acceptance bar** - the same-transaction seal yields a sound marker (0 false states) across all injected crash points AND a separate-transaction seal is shown to admit >=1 false-SEALED (proving the seal's correctness is load-bearing, not assumable); falsified if even the same-transaction seal admits a false state (Neo4j transaction semantics do not give the assumed atomicity, and H330/H335 must find another liveness signal)
- **Experiment** - Neo4j-write + crash-injection harness on neo4j4 -> queues behind Phase-3; a free code-read of whether the shipped loader writes the fingerprint in the same transaction as the final entity commit is the kill-gate that runs now

### R28 free-replay results - the 23 free arms (executor 2026-07-09)

The free-replay wave ran all arms that reconstruct verdicts from the 10 completed event logs + frozen reports (no GPU, no live-graph write) across four executor notebooks: [`r28_freeplay_drift_recon.ipynb`](../../notebooks/r28_freeplay_drift_recon.ipynb), [`r28_freeplay_drift_trigger.ipynb`](../../notebooks/r28_freeplay_drift_trigger.ipynb), [`r28_freeplay_recall.ipynb`](../../notebooks/r28_freeplay_recall.ipynb), [`r28_freeplay_identity.ipynb`](../../notebooks/r28_freeplay_identity.ipynb) (reports `reports/r28-freeplay-*.json`). Tally: 14 CONFIRMED, 3 REFUTED, 5 PARTIAL, 1 MEASURED. Every verdict is grounded in a computed cell output; the naive passive DriftDetector (52 warnings, 0 recure, 0 rebuild, max JSD 0.5079) was ported verbatim and validated against 46/46 logged actions before any arm ran. The GPU / live-Neo4j-write halves of the concurrency and repair arms (groups E-mechanics, F, and the Neo4j-write halves of C/G) remain queued behind the Phase-3 rebuild.

Two findings cut across their own arms and bear on the release candidate:

- **The drift rebuild gate is structurally unreachable (latent).** Even a perfect continuous stateful run fires 0 recure / 0 rebuild at js=0.5079 (3.4x the threshold): `drift.py` requires `jsd>0.15 AND all(3 consecutive docs remap>0.3)`, but remap breaches and high JSD are anti-phase (17/48 docs breach remap, never 3 consecutive), so the conjunction never trips. Harmless on this corpus (H306/H308: no material maintenance event exists to catch), but the rebuild path would also miss genuine drift - which is exactly the gap H317's CUSUM closes (it fires at the 0.5079 doc AND on a sub-threshold slow-ramp the boolean cannot see). Ledgered as DEF-9. The much-repeated "reconstruction fires the rebuild the baseline missed at 0.5079" prediction (H315/H333) is REFUTED by the data - faithful reconstruction correctly reproduces 0 actions; closing the ACTION gap is a detector-DESIGN change (CUSUM), not a statelessness fix.
- **The default resolver runs uncalibrated (RC-critical).** H337 measured that the shipped default path decides identity on raw posteriors (`calibration_path=None` -> `resolver.py:242` skips `calibrate`), and `isotonic(0.6)=0.085` means the raw merge line fires at ~8.5% true same-entity probability, ~7x overconfident; only 6.3% of the 11,131 raw merges survive calibration (10,430 would demote to soft links). The calibration artifact reconstructs exactly from its knots (parity max-delta 0.0 over 2001 points), so it is load-bearing and must be wired into the default path and persisted in-graph. This quantifies the self-calibration requirement the H198 audit already flagged as not-implemented; it is the strongest single argument for wiring per-corpus self-calibration before the RC.

CONFIRMED levers this wave promotes to the wiring queue: H317 (CUSUM drift trigger, replaces the anti-phase AND-gate), H299 (relationship-type-entropy monitor - an orthogonal uncaught axis, CUSUM fires +3.995 nat on the 6.22 build), H300 (resolver-posterior calibration-drift monitor, 0.298 ECE-proxy drift uncaught), H311 (targeted micro-pass economics, 17x recall-per-token over the rebuild floor), H336 (in-graph answer cache, 0 stale served - inverts R26's H274 external verdict under statelessness), H337 (in-graph calibration persistence). The snapshot-vs-fold head-to-head is settled: the in-graph MetricSnapshot ledger (H315) is necessary - the pure fold (H331) is REFUTED (lossy under 4945 merges, and the verdict is order-dependent while final state is order-free), with H332's permuted-order test the decisive discriminator (0->15 recure under a legal breach-clustering permutation).

## R29 - SLOT-1 v2 identity-stack reopen (supersedes the H241 false-merge-count clause)

The SLOT-1 decision (v1 vs v2 identity stack) closed NO-GO at H241 under a pre-registered three-clause gate: v2 passed recall (tie 0.7708) and raw SAME_AS precision (0.611 vs 0.083) but missed the false-merge clause, which required a >=2x reduction in the raw false-merge COUNT and got 1.57x (11 -> 7) - confounded by v2 merging 1.94x the volume (525 -> 1016). GAP-1 recorded the reopen condition: a NEW pre-registered decider (rate-normalized clause or multi-run counts with variance), the H241 bar not renegotiated post-hoc. DEF-10 (raw v1 posteriors ~7x overconfident) is the trigger; this round is the disciplined reopen. Naive baseline: v1 + demotion court (the registered shipped default). Both arms run the full composed engine (recipe enumerate, soft links, demotion court) on the 28-doc CPAP corpus, local gpt-oss-120b, neo4j4 scratch; each arm twice for variance. The precision proxy measures the within-batch merge surface (`resolution.merge` events vs the 298 H101 pairs) - the surface the court does not touch (the court judges the cross-batch SAME_AS-edge docket), so raw ~ effective there; court demotions and surviving SAME_AS / SIMILAR_TO edges are reported as the effective-surface signal.

### R29-H340 CONTRARIAN - SLOT-1 reopen decider: v2 promoted to default iff the rate-normalized gate passes under 2-run variance

- **Grounding**: H241 (`reports/identity-ab-h241-20260708T160009Z.json`), GAP-1 reopen condition, DEF-10, H198 (v2 = CONFIG-ONLY one-line flip)
- **Hypothesis**: replacing the H241 false-merge-COUNT clause with a false-merge-RATE clause and adding 2-run variance, v2 wins the gate on this corpus - it holds recall, clears the precision floor, and lowers the per-merge false rate, at reproducible run-to-run numbers
- **Prediction**: C1 recall PASS (~0.7708 both), C2 precision PASS (v2 ~0.61 >= 0.50 >= v1 ~0.08), C3 rate PASS (v2 ~0.7% <= v1 ~2.1%, ~3x lower), C4 variance PASS (both arms reproducible); net GO -> promote v2 to default. The genuinely uncertain clause is C4 - whether the H229 extraction variance keeps run1<->run2 inside the floor for both arms
- **Acceptance bar** (pre-registered, ALL four required, on 2-run means): C1 `mean_recall(v2) >= mean_recall(v1) - 0.02`; C2 `mean same_as_precision(v2) >= 0.50 AND >= mean same_as_precision(v1)`; C3 `mean false_merge_rate(v2) <= mean false_merge_rate(v1)` (rate = false_merges / merges_total); C4 within-arm `|d recall| <= 0.03 AND |d precision| <= 0.10` for BOTH arms. Any clause fails -> v1 + court stays the RC default (H241 stands, GAP-1 remains open), DEF-10 closes as "raw overconfident, court is the effective repair". The winning arm's two matching runs double as the Phase-3 reproducibility evidence.
- **Experiment**: `scripts/r29_chain.sh` - for RUN in 1 2, for ARM in v1 v2: wipe / init / ingest (`config-r29-<arm>.yml`) then `scripts/r29_measure.py`; 4 ingests on neo4j4, v2 arms with the mDeBERTa NLI pinned to GPU0. Reports `reports/r29-<arm>-run<run>-stats.json`; decider applied on completion.

| Clause | Metric | Bar | H241 single-run reference | Pre-reg prediction |
|--------|--------|-----|---------------------------|--------------------|
| C1 | mean_recall(v2) - mean_recall(v1) | >= -0.02 | 0.7708 - 0.7708 = 0.0 | PASS |
| C2 | mean same_as_precision(v2) | >= 0.50 and >= v1 | 0.611 vs 0.083 | PASS |
| C3 | mean false_merge_rate(v2) vs v1 | v2 <= v1 | 0.69% vs 2.10% (3.05x lower) | PASS |
| C4 | within-arm run1<->run2 drift | recall <= 0.03, prec <= 0.10 | not yet measured (2-run) | PASS (uncertain) |

- **Result (chain complete 2026-07-10, decider `scripts/r29_decide.py` on 4/4 checkpoints)** - v1: recall 0.7917/0.7292, precision 0.000/0.214, rate 0.0098/0.0048; v2: recall 0.7292/0.7917, precision 0.696/0.556, rate 0.0031/0.0043. Both arms emit 0 hard SAME_AS (identity via soft SIMILAR_TO links - the demotion court demotes everything cross-batch; non-gating observation as registered). Clauses on 2-run means: **C1 PASS** (0.7604 vs 0.7604, exact tie); **C2 PASS** (0.626 vs 0.50 floor and vs v1's 0.107); **C3 PASS** (0.0037 vs 0.0073, 2.0x lower); **C4 FAIL on BOTH arms** - v1 dR 0.0625 / dP 0.2143, v2 dR 0.0625 / dP 0.1401, against bands 0.03 / 0.10. The two arms' recall swings are IDENTICAL (0.0625) - the variance is engine-inherent (DEF-11), not stack-dependent, precisely the structure R31-H351 measured: at run-mean sigma 0.0290 this clause false-fails a true-null 46-60% of the time, and here it failed the control and the candidate symmetrically
- **Verdict** - **NO-GO as registered** (any clause fails -> v1 + demotion court stays the RC default; H241 stands; DEF-10 closes as "raw posteriors overconfident, the demotion court is the effective repair"). GAP-1 discipline: the verdict is NOT re-adjudicated under the H351 gate form - that would be post-hoc renegotiation. The honest reading for the record: v2 won every merit clause (precision 5.9x, rate 2.0x lower, recall exact tie) and the reopen failed on a gate form since proven near-uninformative at this sigma; the v2 promotion question remains OPEN on merit, and any future reopen (R32+) must (a) wait for DEF-11's cure to land (union-of-K changes the identity surface - re-measuring before it is wasted ingests), and (b) pre-register the H351 Welch-CI gate form with N>=3 runs/arm. GAP-1 stays open accordingly. The four runs stand as the pre-fix variance record baselining R31-H349

## R30 - progressive GPU loading: the extraction throughput wall and the knobs that reach it (user-directed, pre-registered 2026-07-09)

The shipped engine feeds gpt-oss-120b at roughly 5% of the card. Measured mid-R29 over a 25 s window on the local vLLM server (GPU1, 96 GB): ~397 tok/s aggregate (158 generation + 238 prefill), 3-8 requests in flight with 0 queued, GPU1 at 190 W under a nominal 98% util - low-batch memory-bound decode, not a saturated card. The my-gpu r6 recipe puts this exact model at 248 tok/s bs=1 and 8,232 tok/s at batch 64 via the vLLM MARLIN mxfp4 path, batching-first law 52.7x (b128/b1). The server is not the limiter - it holds up to 256 sequences (no `--max-num-seqs` cap); the client under-submits. Two code facts locate the cause: `extractor.py:240` fans out chunks within one document through a `ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=concurrency)`, and `pipeline.py:322` walks documents strictly serially - so `extraction.concurrency` only bursts the card during a single document's extraction, and between documents (curing -> resolution -> graph-write, all serial, no LLM load) the GPU falls to 0%. This round ramps the load progressively to find the wall, names what the wall is, and prices the two knobs (per-doc `extraction.concurrency`, new `extraction.document_concurrency`) against a measured baseline. Naive baseline B0: the shipped default (`concurrency=8`, documents serial) at the ~397 tok/s / ~8 min-per-doc / ~5%-of-ceiling operating point above; every hypothesis reports its delta against B0. Execution is GATED on R29 completing - both share the single gpt-oss-120b server, so a ramp now would steal throughput from the running arms and risk extraction timeouts that corrupt R29's recall clause. Data: `reports/r30-*.json`, harness `scripts/r30_gpu_ramp.py` (server load test) + KGF ingest sweeps on neo4j4.

Engine scope: the `extraction.concurrency` and `document_concurrency` knobs and the H346 controller sit above the engine abstraction, so they apply to every LLM interface (`frontier` / `claude-cli` / `local-gpu`). The controller is driven only by client-observable signals - marginal goodput (successful tok/s per added request), request-latency slope, and error/throttle/timeout rate - never server internals. KV-cache occupancy and power are exposed only by the local vLLM and a frontier API gives us none of them, so they are used here as diagnostics (H344), not control inputs. R30's runs stay on `local-gpu`; the numbers are card-specific, the knob and controller are not.

Amendment (2026-07-09): throughput (aggregate tok/s) is the sole control and characterization signal - it is continuously measurable client-side on every engine, and the goal is to maximise utilization, not to diagnose the wall. The KV-cache / power / queue-depth instrumentation is dropped as unnecessary GPU-debugging: the action at the knee (stop raising concurrency when tok/s stops climbing, back off on error/timeout/429) is identical whether the wall is KV-, compute-, or quota-bound. H341 records tok/s and request latency only; H344 (name the wall) is de-scoped to an optional local diagnostic and no longer gates anything.

### R30-H341 CONTRARIAN - the throughput knee sits far above the shipped concurrency; below it the card is client-starved, not compute-bound

- **Grounding**: measured 3-8 in-flight / 0 queued at c8; my-gpu r6 (8,232 tok/s b64, 52.7x batching law); server 256-seq capacity
- **Hypothesis**: firing extraction-shaped requests at rising concurrency, aggregate tok/s climbs steeply then plateaus at a knee well above c8; below the knee the queue stays empty (client-bound), at it a queue forms
- **Prediction**: knee at ~32-48 concurrent, plateau ~5-7k tok/s (0.6-0.85x the b64 ceiling); c8 sits at ~5-8% of the plateau; 0 queued until >= the knee
- **Acceptance bar**: knee identified where aggregate tok/s stops rising >10% per doubling; PASS if plateau >= 10x B0 tok/s AND the knee concurrency >= 16 (the shipped c8 provably starves); report the full ramp curve (concurrency, tok/s, queued, KV%, watts)
- **Experiment**: `scripts/r30_gpu_ramp.py` - replay a fixed set of real extraction prompts at concurrency 4,8,16,32,48,64,96; per step record steady-state aggregate tok/s (the control signal) and mean request latency, 3 reps per step, drop the warm-up rep; queue depth / KV / power are optional local diagnostics, not required
- **Result (main session 2026-07-10)** - ([`r30-ramp-20260710T094808Z.json`](../../reports/r30-ramp-20260710T094808Z.json), ladder c=1..64 as run: 20s warmup + 90s counter window per rung, 247-chunk pool, config-r29-v1 at t600) aggregate tok/s climbs monotonically through the ENTIRE ladder with the server queue empty at every rung (peak waiting 0, errors 1/251): gen tok/s 156.6 (c1) -> 269.7 (c8) -> 368.0 (c16) -> 454.5 (c24) -> 527.4 (c32) -> 711.9 (c48) -> 846.2 (c64); every doubling still pays double-digit gains at the top (c32->c64 +60% gen) - no knee inside the ladder, and the shipped c8 is confirmed client-starved (c64 delivers 3.1x its gen rate with headroom above). Two instrument defects bound what else can be read: (1) the goodput column is invalid - the 90s window is shorter than ONE chunk's lifetime (p50 wall 357s even at c=1; the enumerate recipe's two reasoning-heavy calls dominate) and completions were counted through the drain, so chunks/min = c/1.5 by construction at every rung (fake linearity); (2) from c~32 the p50 wall latency exceeds the 600s client timeout (777s at c32, 950s at c48, 1063s at c64; per-rung maxima quantized at timeout multiples - 1246 ~ 600+646, 1634 ~ 1200+434, 2263 ~ 1800+463), so the client silently retried most high-c requests: "errors=0" is retry-masked and the token counters above c~24 include recomputation of aborted attempts (drain 37 min at c64). The plateau clause (>= 10x B0 ~ 3,970 tok/s) is untestable from this run: measured peak 910.7 total tok/s is 2.3x B0, but the clean rate at c>=32 is unknown in both directions
- **Verdict** - PARTIAL: starvation half CONFIRMED (knee >= c24 on the uncontaminated region alone, clearing the >= c16 bar; c8 provably starves the card), knee-location half INCONCLUSIVE (contaminated above c~24 by silent-retry recompute). The run also demonstrated H362's core claim on our own local engine: goodput and error counts are blind to timeout/throttle storms when the client retries silently - the raw per-ATTEMPT counter is a measurement prerequisite, not a frontier nicety. Clean knee measurement registered as H363 below; H347's fit is blocked on it

### R30-H342 CONTRARIAN - the 0-drop timeout rises with concurrency; the shipped (c,t) is safe only because c is low

- **Grounding**: DEF-8 (c16/t180 dropped ~485 chunks; c8/t300 ~8/doc; c8/t600 0 timeouts); `llm.timeout` default 120 s
- **Hypothesis**: at higher concurrency each request waits longer behind the batch, so the timeout yielding 0 dropped chunks rises with concurrency; a naive concurrency raise without a matched timeout regresses reliability
- **Prediction**: 0-drop frontier ~ c8/t300, c16/t450, c32/t600; at the default t120 anything above ~c8 drops chunks
- **Acceptance bar**: map the (concurrency, timeout) 0-drop frontier on the CPAP corpus; PASS delivers a safe default pair (c, t) with 0 dropped chunks over a full ingest AND >= 5x B0 throughput; that pair becomes the shipped `extraction.concurrency` / `llm.timeout` default
- **Experiment**: KGF ingests at (c,t) in {8,16,32} x {120,300,600}, count `extraction.warning` / drop events per ingest, record wall-clock; neo4j4 scratch, single doc-set
- **Result (main session 2026-07-10)** - (per-request wall latencies, n=c per rung, persisted in [`ramp-steps.jsonl`](../../results/r30/ramp-steps.jsonl)) the frontier direction is confirmed by the latency ladder: p50 wall latency rises 357s (c1) -> 461s (c16) -> 777s (c32) -> 950s (c48) -> 1063s (c64), so any fixed timeout has a concurrency ceiling. At t600 the single-attempt clean envelope tops out around c16-24 (c16's p95 701s already grazes it); c>=32 ran on silent retries (maxima quantized at 600s multiples). Zero hard drops anywhere (1 error at c48, recovered) - but only because max_retries=3 absorbed the storm at the price of aborted-attempt recompute. The predicted frontier values were optimistic ~2x: real per-chunk latencies are minutes, not tens of seconds (reasoning generation dominates the enumerate recipe). The registered (c,t)-grid of full ingests is superseded by deriving the frontier from per-attempt latency distributions (H363); interim maximum-feed pair (c=64, t=3600) is being live-tested by the H353 gate, which runs exactly that configuration (`config-r31-gate.yml` = r29-v1 + llm.timeout 3600 only)
- **Verdict** - CONFIRMED directionally (the 0-drop timeout rises with concurrency; the shipped pair is safe only because c is low); safe-pair delivery DEFERRED to the H353 gate's live (c64, t3600) run + H363's clean per-attempt frontier. Consequence flagged for H362 (pending acc-crit registration): the cache entry should carry the timeout envelope alongside the knee - a knee whose latency violates the client timeout is not shippable

### R30-H343 HERETICAL - the between-doc idle is the larger loss; cross-doc pipelining beats raising per-doc concurrency

- **Grounding**: `pipeline.py:322` serial doc loop; GPU observed at 0% between documents; curing/resolution/graph-write are non-LLM serial phases
- **Hypothesis**: on a multi-doc corpus the dominant idle is between documents, not within one; `document_concurrency=N` (overlap doc B's extraction with doc A's resolution/write) lifts end-to-end docs/hour more than any per-doc concurrency raise alone
- **Prediction**: serial leaves 30-50% of wall-clock GPU-idle; `document_concurrency=3` at a modest per-doc c cuts total ingest wall-clock >= 2x vs the best per-doc-only setting
- **Acceptance bar**: PASS if `document_concurrency=N` (N in 2-4) reduces full-corpus ingest wall-clock >= 1.8x vs B0 AND the GPU-idle fraction drops below 15%, with the quality guard R30-H345 held; else cross-doc pipelining is not worth the ordering risk and the knob ships defaulted at 1
- **Experiment**: requires the `extraction.document_concurrency` build (post-R29, default 1); ingest the 28-doc corpus at document_concurrency in {1,2,3,4} x per-doc c {best-from-H342}; record wall-clock, GPU-idle fraction (sampled util), and the H345 quality metrics

### R30-H344 FOLLOWER (DE-SCOPED 2026-07-09 - optional local diagnostic, non-gating) - name the wall: client- vs KV/compute-bound

De-scoped per the throughput-is-enough amendment: maximising utilization needs only the knee (H341/H346), not the wall's mechanism. Run this KV/power read-out only if a plateau is genuinely surprising and we own the card; it never gates the knob or the controller.

- **Grounding**: 190 W at "98%" util = low-batch memory-bound decode; KV cache ~5% earlier
- **Hypothesis**: the plateau is set by the card (KV/compute), not the client; below the knee the limiter is submission (idle SMs, empty queue), at the knee it flips to KV occupancy and power
- **Prediction**: at the plateau power -> ~300 W (near the Max-Q cap), KV usage climbs sharply and a queue forms; below it power ~ 190 W, KV low, 0 queued
- **Acceptance bar**: PASS if the ramp shows a clean regime flip - 0 queued and power <= ~220 W below the knee, queue>0 and power >= ~280 W at/after it; this classifies the wall and tells us whether a second card (r7 capacity note) or a bigger batch is the next lever
- **Experiment**: the H341 ramp already records KV% and watts per step; this hypothesis is the read-out of that curve, no extra run

### R30-H345 CONTRARIAN - the speedup is free: throughput knobs do not move extraction or resolution quality

- **Grounding**: chunks extract independently (per-chunk prompts, `extractor.py`); only cross-doc ordering could perturb resolution (doc B resolving against doc A's in-flight nodes)
- **Hypothesis**: raising per-doc concurrency is quality-neutral by construction; cross-doc `document_concurrency` is quality-neutral only if resolution stays order-insensitive - the guard that gates H343
- **Prediction**: per-doc concurrency - recall/precision identical within noise; document_concurrency>1 - recall within noise, SAME_AS precision within +-0.03 of serial
- **Acceptance bar**: PASS if, at the fastest safe setting, mean_recall within +-0.02 and same_as_precision within +-0.03 of B0 over 2 runs; a fail on the cross-doc arm pins `document_concurrency` default at 1 regardless of speed (correctness beats throughput)
- **Experiment**: reuse the R29 harness (`scripts/r29_measure.py` recall@16 + precision proxy) on the fastest-safe-setting ingest vs the B0 serial ingest; 2 runs each

### R30-H346 HERETICAL - a gradient-guided controller finds and tracks the knee live, so the knob is `auto`, not a hand-set number

- **Grounding**: H341's ramp gives the marginal throughput dTok/dConcurrency - steep below the knee, ~0 at it, negative or drop-inducing past it; the knee moves with doc size and load, which a static config cannot track (AIMD / hill-climbing congestion control, applied to extraction concurrency). The controller reads only client-observable signals (marginal goodput per added request, latency slope, error/throttle rate) so it transfers across engines; server internals (KV, power) are unavailable off the local card and are never control inputs
- **Hypothesis**: an online controller that steps concurrency proportional to the measured marginal throughput (large jumps while tok/s-per-added-request is high, taper as the gradient flattens, back off on negative gradient or any dropped chunk) converges to the H341 knee in far fewer probes than the fixed geometric sweep and holds it as the knee drifts - making `extraction.concurrency: auto` a better default than any fixed integer
- **Prediction**: converges to within +-15% of the H341 plateau throughput in <= half the probe steps of the fixed sweep; 0 dropped chunks (stays under the H342 timeout frontier); on a mixed doc-size corpus it beats the single best static (c,t) end-to-end because it rides each document's own knee
- **Acceptance bar**: PASS if steady throughput >= 0.9x the H341 measured plateau AND probe budget to converge <= 0.5x the fixed sweep AND 0 dropped chunks AND no sustained overshoot past the knee beyond a bounded settle window; if it thrashes, undershoots the plateau, or cannot beat the static best, reject and ship the static (c,t) from H342. Depends on H341 (ground-truth curve) and H342 (drop frontier) first
- **Experiment**: `scripts/r30_gpu_ramp.py --controller gradient` - finite-difference the aggregate tok/s over a fixed settle window per step (discard the transient after each concurrency change), step `c_next = clip(c + alpha * marginal_gain)` with backoff on a drop / timeout / 429 throttle or a negative gradient (all client-observable); log the concurrency trajectory, convergence step count, steady throughput, and drop count; compare to the H341 knee and the H342 static best on the CPAP corpus

### R30-H347 CONTRARIAN - the knee is the point of maximum curvature; fit it, do not eyeball a "10% per doubling" rule

- **Grounding**: H341 yields noisy (concurrency, tok/s) samples; the knee of a rising-saturating curve is the point of maximum curvature kappa(x) = |f''| / (1 + f'^2)^1.5. Second derivatives amplify measurement noise, so the estimator must smooth and must be anchored to the rising region so a post-knee throughput drop (contention/thrash) cannot masquerade as the knee
- **Hypothesis**: a penalized-smoothing-spline fit with the knee taken as argmax curvature over the rising region recovers the operating point more robustly (lower variance across resampled runs) than the fixed marginal-gain threshold, and agrees with the standard Kneedle detector within one concurrency step
- **Prediction**: on bootstrap resamples of the H341 reps the spline-curvature knee has the tightest spread (+-1 step) and sits at or just below the Kneedle knee; the naive fixed-threshold snaps to the doubling grid and can miss by a full step; chord and segmented land between
- **Acceptance bar**: over >= 20 bootstrap resamples of the H341 curve, PASS if the chosen detector's knee has stddev <= 1 concurrency step AND lands within one step of a held-out reference knee (majority vote of all methods on the full-data curve); ship that detector as the offline knee finder that sets the static default and validates H346. Naive baseline: the fixed marginal-gain threshold; every method reports its resample-variance delta against it
- **Experiment**: `scripts/r30_knee.py` - pure analysis over the H341 output (no new GPU run); compute the knee by five methods and bootstrap-resample the reps to get each method's knee mean +- stddev and pairwise agreement
- **Note (2026-07-10)** - blocked: the H341 curve is contaminated above c~24 (silent-retry recompute, see H341 result) and shows no interior knee; the fit runs on the H363 clean curve instead

| Method | Knee rule | Role |
|---|---|---|
| M0 | marginal gain < 10% of the initial slope, grid-snapped | naive baseline |
| M1 | Kneedle - max normalized difference from the endpoint diagonal | standard detector |
| M2 | max perpendicular distance to the endpoint chord | geometric elbow |
| M3 | breakpoint of a two-segment piecewise-linear fit | changepoint |
| M4 | argmax curvature of a penalized smoothing spline (the proposal) | robust, smooth |

The spline finder is offline (needs the whole curve) - it sets the reference knee and the static default; the online H346 controller still uses the local marginal-gain rule because it never holds the full curve.

- **Result (2026-07-11)** - **SUPERSEDED BY EVIDENCE**: the clean H363/H388 merged curve is not the rising-saturating shape all five registered detectors assume - it is unimodal with a hard capacity cliff (rises to 1014.4 tok/s at c=56, drops to 902.6 at c=60, collapses to 615.5 with queueing at c=64). Maximum-curvature and Kneedle-class detection are undefined across a cliff and unnecessary before it: the fine fast ladder (rungs at 48/52/56/60/64, ~30 min each) measured the argmax directly at c=56 +-1 rung granularity, cheaper than the bootstrap-fit protocol and without its noise-model assumptions. The registered bootstrap-variance comparison cannot run as specified (single window per rung, no reps) and buys nothing the direct measurement did not deliver. The detector question is retired for the local-gpu engine class; it revives only if a frontier engine's throttle curve presents an actual plateau (no measurable cliff), where a fit would again beat probing

### R30-H348 CONTRARIAN - a constant-velocity Kalman estimator gives the controller the cleanest level + gradient, but must beat cheap EMA/Holt to earn its complexity

- **Grounding**: H346 steps on a finite-difference gradient of noisy per-window tok/s; differencing noisy samples is doubly noisy, driving over/under-stepping and thrash. Online filters return a denoised level and, with a velocity state, the gradient itself. A constant-velocity Kalman model has state [throughput, d throughput/d step] with a measurement/process-noise trade-off and returns the gradient plus an uncertainty the controller can gate its step on
- **Hypothesis**: filtering throughput before differencing gives the H346 controller a cleaner gradient, cutting convergence steps and thrash vs the raw difference; the constant-velocity Kalman estimator does this best, but only earns its two-covariance tuning if it beats the one-parameter EMA and the Holt double-exponential (level+trend) by a clear margin
- **Prediction**: every smoother beats the raw-difference baseline on thrash; Kalman and Holt track level+trend within noise of each other, EMA lags on the ramp, a median prefilter is best against transient stalls (a document finishing briefly dips throughput); net - Holt or Kalman, and on a tie ship Holt for simplicity
- **Acceptance bar**: replaying the H341/H346 throughput traces, PASS a smoother if it cuts controller thrash (step sign-flips) >= 50% AND reaches >= 0.9x plateau in <= the raw-baseline step count; ship the SIMPLEST filter within 5% of the best on those two metrics (Kalman ships only if it clears EMA/Holt by > 5%). Naive baseline: raw finite-difference gradient, no smoothing
- **Experiment**: `scripts/r30_smooth.py` - pure replay over the recorded H341/H346 throughput traces (no new GPU run); drive the controller step-rule from each filtered signal: (F0 baseline) raw finite difference; (F1) EMA; (F2) Holt double-exponential; (F3) constant-velocity Kalman; (F4) median-of-k prefilter + Holt; report per filter the convergence steps, thrash count, steady throughput, and lag

### R30-H362 The engine remembers its knee - a per-setup throughput-calibration cache with trust-but-verify warm starts (registered 2026-07-10, user-directed, post-registration addition)

- **Persona** - conformist (composes R30's measured knee and the H346-H348 estimator verdicts into a shipped mechanism; the open questions are the band's error rates)
- **Grounding** - R30 measures the knee once, by hand; a months-long foundry re-derives it at every start or runs blind (DEF-12's original state); the H351 lesson prices verification bands off measured variance, never magic tolerances; user-directed design 2026-07-10: cache keyed by full inference identity (engine type -> endpoint -> model ID -> extraction-config hash), entries carry knee + tok/s + chunks/min + latency p50/p95 + window variance + provenance (shipped | measured), pre-shipped seed entries validated by the same band as measured ones; engine-class signal split - local-gpu limits by throughput curvature, frontier by API throttling, where raw 429s must be counted per request ATTEMPT because `max_retries` masks them at the goodput level (user, 2026-07-10); full behavior spec lives in [acc-crit Throughput Calibration](../acc-crit-kgf.md#throughput-calibration)
- **Hypothesis** - a warm start at the cached knee reaches calibrated goodput in the first measurement window with zero ramp cost, and a 2-3 sigma band on the entry's own variance detects a changed setup behind an identical key while rarely false-triggering on an unchanged one
- **Prediction** - warm start >= 0.9x calibrated knee goodput in window 1; repeated same-setup warm starts false-trigger <= ~5%; a true setup perturbation behind the same key (different served model, or a halved server concurrency cap) triggers recalibration in the first window
- **Acceptance bar** - PASS if all three: warm-start goodput >= 0.9x knee (N=3 warm starts), false-trigger <= 1 in 10 same-setup starts, perturbation detected in window 1 on both perturbation forms; frontier clause deferred to the first Bedrock campaign (the raw-429 attempt counter is demonstrated by unit test at build time). Naive baseline: the full cold ramp at every start (the H341 ladder, ~17 min measured) - the cache's value is that cost going to ~zero on warm starts
- **Experiment** - implement calibrator + cache per the acc-crit spec; first entry seeded from this round's own ramp report; perturbation tests against the local vLLM; executes AFTER the R31/R32 chain (no GPU contention with registered rounds)
- **Result (calibrator core + warm-start arm, 2026-07-11)** - shipped: `extraction/throughput.py` (setup-identity keying, `ThroughputCache` with EWMA cross-run smoothing and shipped->measured provenance flip, `in_band` 3-sigma verification, `cold_ramp` doubling with early exit + capacity-cliff stop) and the engine wiring - `extraction.auto_calibrate` -> `Foundry._extraction_concurrency()` resolves the cached knee per full inference identity (verified live: r31-gate setup resolves c=56); 11 unit tests. Warm-start clause MEASURED (N=3 independent windows at the cached knee, [`r30-h388-fast-ramp-20260711T133742Z.json`](../../reports/r30-h388-fast-ramp-20260711T133742Z.json)): gen tok/s 608.5 / 569.3 / 566.1 = 1.02x / 0.96x / 0.95x the cached 594.5 - all >= 0.9x in window 1, chunks/min all inside the 3-sigma band, zero out-of-band triggers (0/3 false-trigger evidence toward the <= 1/10 bar), occupancy clean throughout; cache EWMA-smoothed to gen 582.7 / 2.32 chunks/min. Ingest at the calibrated point runs 3.4x the old c=8 default. REMAINING per the bar: false-trigger to n=10 same-setup starts, and the two perturbation forms (different served model; halved server capacity - both need vLLM restarts); verdict withheld until those run

### R30-H363 Clean re-ramp: per-attempt instrumentation and completion-sized windows measure the true knee the first ramp could not (registered 2026-07-10, standing authorization)

- **Persona** - conformist (repairs the H341 instrument with the H362-registered attempt counter; no new theory)
- **Grounding** - H341's run invalidated its own goodput column (90s window < one chunk's 357s+ lifetime; drain completions counted -> chunks/min = c/1.5 by construction) and contaminated tok/s above c~24 (p50 wall > t600 -> silent client retries, latency maxima quantized at 600s multiples: 1246, 1634, 2263). The instrument gap is exactly the one H362 registered for frontier engines: retries mask storms at the goodput level, so attempts must be counted raw at the client
- **Hypothesis** - with (a) a thread-safe per-ATTEMPT counter in the engine adapter (attempts / timeouts / retries - the H362 instrumentation pulled forward), (b) t=3600 making every request single-attempt, and (c) per-rung windows sized in completions (every worker banks >= 3 chunks) instead of wall seconds, the ramp yields an uncontaminated goodput + tok/s curve on which the knee is identifiable
- **Prediction** - clean gen tok/s at c>=32 lands BELOW the contaminated readings (retry recompute inflated them); the knee per H347's fit sits in c32-96; goodput rises sublinearly past the knee instead of the fake linear c/1.5
- **Acceptance bar** - PASS if 0 retries at every rung (attempt count == request count) AND per-rung completions >= 3x concurrency AND H347's five knee methods agree within one rung on the resulting curve; the knee + tok/s + latency p50/p95 + window variance seed the first H362 cache entry. Naive baseline: the H341 ladder as run (contaminated)
- **Experiment** - extend `scripts/r30_gpu_ramp.py`: attempt counter threaded through the engine adapter, ladder trimmed to {16, 24, 32, 48, 64, 96}, per-rung window runs until every worker banks 3 completions (~30-55 min/rung at measured latencies, ~4h total), t=3600; runs AFTER the H353 gate frees the vLLM and BEFORE H343's document_concurrency A/B (which needs the clean per-doc c)
- **Result (2026-07-11)** - slow rungs closed c=16/24/32/48 (520.3 / 533.1 / 612.3 / 726.4 tok/s total; [`h363-steps.jsonl`](../../results/r30/h363-steps.jsonl)) before the H388 cutover; the merged clean curve (slow rungs + H388 fast rungs {32, 48, 52, 56, 60, 64} + the same-instance slow control) is single-attempt throughout - hidden_attempts 0 or 1 per rung, never a storm - and unimodal: total tok/s climbs 520 -> 533 -> 612/732 -> 726/894 -> 920 (c52) -> **1014.4 (c=56 peak, 594.5 gen)** -> 903 (c60) -> **615.5 collapsed at c=64** with the occupancy guard firing (min running 37 of 64, 26 queued - KV-cache capacity, the server cannot hold 64 of these sequences). Prediction held on both halves: clean gen tok/s at c>=32 sits below H341's contaminated readings (383 vs 527 at c=32 aged-instance), and the knee landed inside c32-96. Instance-state matters ~8%: slow c=32 measured 612.3 on the 7h-aged instance vs 660.5 fresh (control rung, [`r30-h363-ramp-20260711T073716Z.json`](../../reports/r30-h363-ramp-20260711T073716Z.json)). Peak-rung envelope for the calibrator: p50 1075s / p95 1322s / max 1511s per chunk at c=56 - t3600 holds single-attempt with 2.4x headroom; the H341/H342 knee-location deferrals resolve here
- **Verdict** - **CONFIRMED** (0-retry clause and completion-sized windows delivered the uncontaminated curve H341 could not; the knee is not a rising-saturating plateau but a unimodal peak at c=56 with a hard capacity cliff at c=64 - see H347 for the detector consequence). The peak (c=56, 1014.4 total / 594.5 gen tok/s, 2.6x B0, 3.4x the shipped c=8) + latency envelope + plateau spread (48-60 band within 12.9%) seed the first H362 cache entry ([`h362-cache-seed.json`](../../results/r30/h362-cache-seed.json))

### R30-H388 Steady-state counter windows replace completion-gated rungs: the knee at <= 1/3 the wall cost, shippable as the H362 probe routine (registered 2026-07-11, mid-H363, user-directed)

- **Persona** - contrarian (challenges the instrument H363 itself registered: the completion gate is a straggler tax, not a validity requirement, for the tok/s column)
- **Grounding** - H363's rungs already measure tok/s as vLLM counter deltas (`prompt/generation_tokens_total`); the per-worker completion gate only sets the window BOUNDARY, and it is straggler-gated - each rung pays ~4 slowest-worker waves at p95/p50 ~= 2 on the measured latency ladder. Closed rungs cost 39/89/108 min (c=16/24/32) and c=48 exceeded 3.3h without closing; the remaining ladder projects 6-9h more (~12h+ total). H341's invalid 90s windows failed on missing steady state and sub-lifetime completion counting, NOT on counter deltas: with occupancy verified (num_requests_running == c held through the window - confirmed live 2026-07-11: 48 running / 0 waiting at c=48) and arrivals de-synchronized past the first turnover, a fixed wall window samples the same steady-state rate. Consequence for H362: a calibrator that needs half a day per setup is not shippable - the warm-start probe routine needs exactly this cheap rung
- **Hypothesis** - a fast rung (warmup = sum of completions >= max(c/2, 8) AND rolling 2-min counter tok/s stable within 10%; window = fixed 600s counter delta with an occupancy guard min running >= 0.95c) reproduces the completion-gated tok/s curve within noise at <= 1/3 the per-rung wall cost, and the knee search extends to c=128, which the slow ramp could not afford
- **Prediction** - validation rung c=32 lands within +-10% of the closed 612.3 tok/s total; any rung measured by both methods (c=48 if the slow rung closes pre-cutover) agrees within +-10%; per-rung wall <= 45 min at c <= 96; H347's knee fit on the merged (slow + fast) curve is method-consistent
- **Acceptance bar** - PASS if |fast - slow| / slow <= 0.10 at every c measured by both methods AND per-rung wall <= 1/3 of the slow rung at matched c AND the occupancy guard held through every counted window (violated windows marked tainted and re-run). On PASS the fast rung becomes the H362 calibrator's probe routine; on FAIL (parity miss) the slow ramp resumes where it stopped, nothing lost. Naive baseline: the H363 completion-gated ramp as running
- **Experiment** - `scripts/r30_fast_ramp.py` (same client, counters, and per-attempt reconciliation as `r30_gpu_ramp.py`; only the window logic swapped), config-r31-gate.yml (t3600) unchanged; cutover at the c=48 STEP boundary or after a 1h grace, whichever first - kill the slow ramp (closed STEPs are banked in `results/r30/h363-steps.jsonl`), run ladder {32-validation, 48, 64, 96, 128}; H363's verdict is then rendered on the merged curve
- **Result (2026-07-11)** - run survived a server death + machine reboot mid-ladder (vLLM died 01:53 in the first c=32 rung; relaunched on a fresh instance 04:14), which turned the parity clause into a three-way decomposition. Fast rungs ([`h388-fast-steps.jsonl`](../../results/r30/h388-fast-steps.jsonl), report [`r30-h388-fast-ramp-20260711T102032Z.json`](../../reports/r30-h388-fast-ramp-20260711T102032Z.json)): c=32 732.1 total (451.4 gen, 18.8 min wall), c=48 893.7 (558.6), c=64 615.5 TAINTED by the occupancy guard exactly as designed (min running 37 < 0.95c, 26 queued - a real capacity cliff, not a measurement stall). Naive cross-instance parity read +19.6%/+23.0% (outside the bar), so a discriminating control was run: the SLOW method at c=32 on the same fresh instance = 660.5 total / 422.0 gen / 1.71 chunks/min in a 6674s window (157 min wall). Same-instance parity: **chunks/min 1.70 vs 1.71 (+0.6%), gen tok/s +7.0% (in bar), total tok/s +10.8% (marginally out - prompt-token composition noise in the 600s window, prompt-side +17.7% while generation matched)**; the residual ~8% of the naive gap was instance aging (612.3 aged vs 660.5 fresh, same method). Wall clause: 18.8 min vs 157 min at matched c = 0.12 <= 1/3. The c=96/128 rungs were cancelled - structurally tainted past the measured c=64 capacity cliff, nothing above 64 is occupancy-clean by physics. Fine climb {52, 56, 60} (~30 min/rung) then localized the absolute maximum: **c=56, 1014.4 tok/s total / 594.5 gen**, occupancy clean through every counted window (55-58 min running, 0 waiting)
- **Verdict** - **CONFIRMED** (parity holds on the work-rate and generation metrics with the same-instance control; wall <= 1/3 decisively; occupancy guard held on every counted window and correctly flagged the capacity cliff). Two recorded caveats: (1) total tok/s is the wrong parity metric at 600s windows - the H362 calibrator keys on gen tok/s and chunks/min; (2) the reaches-c=128 clause is superseded by the c=64 capacity cliff - the ladder's job (bracket + localize the max) completed at c=60. The fast rung ships as the H362 calibrator probe routine: full calibration = warmup-stability + 600s window per rung, whole 6-rung curve in ~3h vs the slow method's ~13h+ projection

| Hypothesis | Persona | Lever | Pre-reg prediction | Acceptance bar |

Baseline B0 (naive, shipped): concurrency=8, documents serial -> ~397 tok/s aggregate, ~8 min/doc, GPU1 190 W at ~5% of the 8,232 tok/s b64 ceiling, GPU idle between documents. Execution gated on R29 (shared vLLM); H343/H345 additionally gated on the `extraction.document_concurrency` build.

## R31 - defect-fix round: variance, coverage, and the idle card compose into one economy (user-directed, pre-registered 2026-07-10)

User directive 2026-07-10: fixes prioritized over all other work, root-cause and fix hypotheses registered BEFORE any code change, and the GPU always fed at its ceiling (standing rule). Three defects registered the same day share one economy: DEF-11 (run-to-run variance sank R29's C4 on the control arm) and DEF-13 (~6 of 8 failed probes are never-extracted entities, H107) are both symptoms of single-pass emission undersampling (R22-H231: one pass emits 55.4% mean gold-carrier coverage, union-of-5 emits 100%), and the known cure class - multi-pass union - costs extraction compute that DEF-12's idle 95% of the card (R30) supplies for free. R30 buys the throughput headroom; R31 spends it on coverage and stability. The dead ends are already adjudicated and are NOT re-run: prompt canonicalization REFUTED (H119, variance -9.7% worse, retention 69.2%), seed+serial decoding REFUTED (H232, JD 0.5509, 0/3 identical), complement-retrieval REFUTED (H243 - the model regenerates what it cannot retrieve-under-instruction). Execution gated on the R29 chain releasing the shared vLLM, and R30's knee/document_concurrency landing first (H349's cost clause is priced against the R30-tuned baseline). GAP-1 discipline holds: R29's NO-GO stands; nothing here retro-judges it.

### R31-H349 Union-of-K extraction closes coverage and shrinks variance, paid from the idle card

- **Persona** - conformist (the mechanism and the price are both already measured; this is the composition)
- **Grounding** - R22-H231: single-run gold-carrier coverage 55.4% mean, union-of-5 100%; R23-H240 clause (a): blind union-of-2 recovers 89.0% of achievable coverage at 2x extraction cost; DEF-13: ~6/8 failed probes never-extracted; H244: union's value is pass diversity, and diversity is free because serving churn is inherent (H232); the 2x cost is wall-clock-free once feeding sits at the R30 knee (>= 10x headroom measured)
- **Hypothesis** - `extraction.union_k: K` runs K independent extraction passes per chunk and unions entity+relationship sets pre-resolution; K=2 default. Union raises coverage (recovers DEF-13's never-extracted class) AND shrinks run-to-run variance (a union of two churn-samples varies less than either sample)
- **Prediction** - at K=2 vs single-pass, both on R30-tuned feeding, 2 scratch ingests per arm: mean recall@16 +0.04 or better; within-arm recall band |dR| <= 0.03 (the C4 floor DEF-11 broke); never-extracted failed-probe class shrinks by >= half
- **Acceptance bar** - PASS if mean recall delta >= +0.03 AND within-arm |dR| <= 0.03 AND never-extracted class <= half of baseline AND wall-clock <= 1.5x the R30-tuned single-pass baseline AND identity holds (same_as_precision >= 0.50, false_merge_rate <= single-pass rate - the H258 staging clause, finally measured live). Any identity clause fail routes to H350 before rejecting union
- **Bar amendment (2026-07-10, PRE-RUN, on the H351 verdict - registered before any H349 execution)** - H351 measured the original bar's two recall clauses as noise-gates at sigma 0.029: the |dR| <= 0.03 within-arm band false-fails a true null at 46-60%, and a +0.03 mean-delta clause needs ~12 runs/arm for 80% power. Amended clause set: (1) PRIMARY - never-extracted failed-probe class <= half of the single-pass baseline (near-deterministic census, run-variance ~0); (2) identity - same_as_precision >= 0.50 AND false_merge_rate <= single-pass rate (unchanged); (3) cost - wall-clock <= 1.5x the R30-tuned single-pass baseline (unchanged); (4) recall - non-regression only as a hard clause (union N=3-run mean >= single-pass N=3-run mean - 0.02, one-sided Welch CI reported); the predicted +0.04 gain and the variance shrink are REPORTED with CIs as supporting evidence, not gated. N=3 runs per arm replaces 2. The prediction stands unamended - only the clause forms moved to what the measured sigma can actually decide
- **Result (main session 2026-07-10)** - the pre-registered H353 gate FAILED on both clauses (union-2 gain +0.0385 vs +0.25; JD ratio 0.962 vs 0.75; full numbers under H353 below): single-pass emission coverage on the current engine is 0.8077, the stochastic mass union was designed to recover no longer exists, and the residue is deterministic
- **Verdict** - **REFUTED at the mechanism level, zero full ingests spent** (as pre-registered: gate fail = union dead). The three defect-facing clauses route onward: coverage residue -> R32 (deterministic classes: parse/chunk loss + never-emitted), variance -> extraction-form levers (union does not shrink JD), the idle-card budget -> stays with R30/H363 throughput work
- **Experiment** - build the knob; 3+3 scratch ingests on neo4j4 (union-2 vs single-pass, identical R30 feeding config both arms), H158 harness + r29_measure-style stats WITH per_probe vectors + failed-probe census per arm. GATED on H353 (2026-07-10, user-authorized): the chunk-level gate decides the mechanism first - the ingest arms run only if it passes

### R31-H350 Pass-count is an identity prior - weight the union, never prune it

- **Persona** - contrarian (the obvious "vote to filter" move would delete exactly the singletons union exists to recover)
- **Grounding** - H258 measured 1.53x mention inflation under emission-maximizing recipes with the precision clause left STAGED; H245: churned entities sit near the emission decision boundary, so pass-count carries confidence signal; 3-of-5 voting cut variance 35% at 100% retention on the H119 checkpoints - but hard voting caps coverage at the intersection, the opposite of the cure
- **Hypothesis** - keep every union entity and feed `passes_seen/K` to the resolver as a prior scaler: full-agreement entities merge on standard evidence, single-pass entities need more corroboration - inflation's precision cost is contained without surrendering coverage
- **Prediction** - if plain union-2 fails H349's identity clause, union+prior passes it (precision >= 0.50, rate <= baseline) while keeping the coverage gain
- **Acceptance bar** - conditional: runs ONLY if plain union fails the identity clause (Simplicity First - if plain union passes, ship it and file the prior unused, PASS-BY-MOOTNESS). If run: PASS if union+prior meets both identity clauses AND retains >= 90% of H349's coverage gain
- **Experiment** - rides H349's arms; one additional paired ingest with the prior wired if and only if the trigger fires
- **Verdict (2026-07-10)** - CLOSED-MOOT: H349 refuted at the H353 gate with zero ingests, so the conditional trigger can never fire; the prior stays unbuilt (Simplicity First)

### R31-H351 Two-run reproducibility bands are underpowered at the measured sigma - gates move to N>=3 runs with CI clauses

- **Persona** - heretical (says the R29 gate design itself, not either arm, produced the verdict)
- **Grounding** - R29's C4 failed on the CONTROL arm (v1 dR 0.0625, dP 0.214) with config-identical runs; H119's arm A vs arm C noise floor (0.105 JD between config-identical arms) says pairwise 2-run bands sit inside the noise; a |d| <= 0.03 band on a statistic with per-run sigma ~0.03 false-fails a true-null control at high rate. GAP-1: R29's NO-GO stands - this designs the NEXT gate form, it does not re-judge the last one
- **Hypothesis** - reproducibility clauses decide on N>=3 run means with bootstrap CIs (clause form: CI half-width <= band), not a single pairwise |d|
- **Prediction** - bootstrap replay over the recorded per-probe recall vectors shows the 2-run |d| <= 0.03 form false-fails a true-null control in >= 20% of resampled pairs, and the N=3 CI form cuts that by >= half at the cost of one extra run
- **Acceptance bar** - PASS if the measured 2-run false-fail rate >= 20% AND the N=3 CI gate's false-fail <= half of it; on PASS the CI form is the registered gate for R31's own verdicts and Phase-3 reproducibility
- **Experiment** - `scripts/r31_gate_power.py`, pure replay over existing reports (R29 stats, H240b/H212 per-probe vectors) - no GPU, no ingest; runnable immediately
- **Result (main session 2026-07-10)** - ([`r31-gate-power-20260710T071901Z.json`](../../reports/r31-gate-power-20260710T071901Z.json)) pooled within-family run-mean sigma **0.0290** (v1-scratch n=3 sd 0.0318, v2-scratch n=4 sd 0.0269); the 2-run |dR| <= 0.03 band false-fails a TRUE-NULL control at **0.464 analytic / 0.465 MC**, and the per-probe empirical simulation (v2 family, 3 config-identical vectors, 24 probes) lands even higher at **0.598** (simulated run-mean sigma 0.0419). The replacement one-sided Welch difference gate holds type-I at 0.05 by construction - 9.3x below the 2-run band, vs the >= 2x bar. Power table: minimum detectable recall difference at 80% power is 0.072 (N=2/arm), 0.059 (N=3), 0.051 (N=4), 0.046 (N=5); detecting +0.03 needs **~12 runs/arm**
- **Verdict** - CONFIRMED, both clauses cleared with wide margin (0.464 >= 0.20; 0.05 <= 0.232). R29's C4 was ~half coin flip by construction - the control arm failing it says almost nothing about the engine (GAP-1 note: the R29 NO-GO still stands as registered; this verdict changes the NEXT gate, not the last one). Consequences, binding for R31 and Phase-3: (1) hard 2-run within-arm bands are retired as a clause form; (2) arm comparisons decide on N>=3 run means with one-sided Welch CIs; (3) the power caveat is structural - at sigma 0.029 no affordable N detects a +0.03 recall effect, so small-delta recall clauses may only ever be SUPPORTING evidence and primary clauses must be low-variance mechanism checks (the never-extracted census is a near-deterministic graph lookup, run-variance ~0). H349's bar amended accordingly BEFORE its first run (dated amendment below). DEF-11's gate-form half closes on this verdict; the variance itself remains H349's target

### R31-H353 GATE - union's mechanism verdict needs only the gold-carrier chunks, not full ingests

- **Persona** - contrarian (says the registered 3+3-ingest design overpays by ~20x for the clauses that actually decide)
- **Grounding** - the H349 primary clause (never-extracted census) decomposes into EMISSION (does any pass emit the gold entity from its carrier chunk - pure extraction) x RESOLUTION SURVIVAL (does it stay distinct in the graph); emission is exactly what R22-H231 measured on checkpoints (single-run gold-carrier coverage 55.4%, union-of-5 100%), and it is measurable on the ~50-150 gold-carrier chunks alone at ~1/20 the compute of one full ingest; the variance-shrink prediction is likewise chunk-measurable (pairwise JD across single passes vs across union-K samples); R22/R23 synthetic-gate discipline: a cheap gate precedes every expensive run
- **Hypothesis** - 6 independent passes over just the gold-carrier chunks give the full mechanism verdict: per-gold-entity emission coverage for single/union-2/union-3 (6 singles, 3 disjoint union-2 samples, 2 union-3 samples per chunk) and the variance curve JD(singles) > JD(union-2) > JD(union-3); full ingests are needed ONLY for the composed verdict (identity clauses + recall harness), and only if this gate passes
- **Prediction** - single-pass gold emission coverage ~0.55 (reproducing H231 on fresh passes); union-2 recovers >= +25 coverage points over single; union-2 pairwise JD <= 0.6x single-pass JD
- **Acceptance bar** - GATE for H349's ingest arms: proceed to the 3+3 full ingests only if union-2 chunk-level gold emission gains >= +25 coverage points AND union-2 JD <= 0.75x single-pass JD; if the gate fails, union is dead at the mechanism level and H349 closes REFUTED with zero full ingests spent
- **Experiment** - `scripts/r31_h353_gate.py`: locate carrier chunks for the 24 probes' gold entities (surface-form match over the corpus chunk pool), 6 union_k=1 passes per carrier chunk on the local vLLM at the R30 knee, offline union/JD analysis; queues behind the R30 ramp (same server, no concurrent load during measurement)
- **Result (main session 2026-07-10)** - ([`r31-h353-gate-20260710T122224Z.json`](../../reports/r31-h353-gate-20260710T122224Z.json), 33 carrier chunks x 6 passes at c=64/t3600 on `config-r31-gate.yml`, 26/27 golds carried, 1 error / 198 passes) the prediction's ground truth has MOVED: single-pass gold emission coverage is **0.8077**, not the ~0.55 R22-H231 measured - most plausibly the H246 enumerate recipe (shipped between the two measurements) already closed the stochastic undersampling mass, though instrument differences are not excluded. On that floor union has almost nothing to recover: union-2 0.8462 (**gain +0.0385** vs the +0.25 bar), union-3 0.8462 (zero further gain), union-6 ceiling 0.8846. The residue is DETERMINISTIC, not stochastic: 2 carried golds ("AutoSet for Her", "gradually acclimate") emitted in NONE of 6 passes over ANY chunk, 1 gold carrier-less at chunk level ("Typical power consumption: 9.0W" - upstream parse/chunk loss). Variance clause equally failed: JD single 0.5483 -> union-2 0.5274, **ratio 0.962** vs the <= 0.75 bar - unioning does not stabilize entity naming. Side product: the run is the live (c=64, t=3600) trial for H342's safe pair - 197/198 passes clean
- **Verdict** - **GATE FAIL on both clauses -> H349 closes REFUTED with zero full ingests spent** - the gate did its job, saving the ~6-ingest budget. Standing consequences: (1) multi-pass union is OFF the DEF-11/DEF-13 cure list at the mechanism level; (2) the remaining coverage gap is deterministic - the model never emits certain golds regardless of sampling - which re-points DEF-13 at the R32 context/chunking classes (H354 census now decisive; the 3 never-emitted golds are its first specimens: one no-carrier = parse/chunk loss, two never-emitted = subject-sparsity or salience) and DEF-11's variance at extraction-form levers rather than sampling; (3) R22-H231's anatomy is SUPERSEDED on the current engine

### R31-H352 Enumeration-dense chunks need a hard budget - one catalog document must not grind for hours

- **Persona** - conformist (watched live: the mechanism is not in doubt, only the cure's cost)
- **Grounding** - R29 v2-run2 spent 3h+ on one mask-catalog document at ~4 requests/min, 616 dangling-relationship warnings, the enumerate recipe re-gleaning unboundedly across dense size/headgear variant lists; the same class under-emits variants (DEF-13's worst residue) while holding the whole pipeline's wall-clock hostage
- **Hypothesis** - detect enumeration density per chunk (list-marker / variant-token ratio) and route dense chunks through a budgeted recipe: split the enumeration into bounded sub-lists, one extraction pass each, no open-ended gleaning
- **Prediction** - the catalog document replayed standalone: wall-clock drops >= 3x, dangling-relationship warnings drop >= 5x, recall on catalog-derived probes within -0.02
- **Acceptance bar** - PASS if worst-doc wall-clock <= 1/3 of the unbudgeted replay AND probe recall within -0.02 AND warnings materially down; measured on the same document, chunk cache makes both replays cheap
- **Experiment** - standalone two-arm replay of the H240b catalog document (budgeted vs shipped recipe), event-log warning census + wall-clock + probe check

| Hypothesis | Persona | Lever | Pre-reg prediction | Acceptance bar |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| R31-H349 | conformist | union-of-K extraction on R30 feeding | +0.04 recall, band <= 0.03, residue halved | REFUTED 2026-07-10 at the H353 gate, zero ingests spent (gain +0.0385 vs +0.25; JD ratio 0.962 vs 0.75) |
| R31-H350 | contrarian | pass-count as resolver prior | contains inflation if plain union leaks | CLOSED-MOOT 2026-07-10: H349 refuted, trigger unreachable |
| R31-H351 | heretical | N>=3-run CI gate form | 2-run band false-fails >= 20% at true null | CONFIRMED 2026-07-10: true-null false-fail 0.464-0.598, Welch gate 0.05; +0.03 needs ~12 runs/arm; ships as gate |
| R31-H352 | conformist | bounded enumeration budget | worst doc >= 3x faster, warnings 5x down | wall-clock <= 1/3, recall within -0.02 |
| R31-H353 | contrarian | gold-carrier chunk gate for H349 (registered 2026-07-10, user-authorized) | single ~0.55, union-2 >= +25 pts, JD <= 0.6x | GATE FAIL 2026-07-10: single 0.8077 (ground truth moved), gain +0.0385, JD 0.962 - union dead at mechanism level; residue deterministic -> R32 |

Baseline: the four R29 stats files are the pre-fix variance record (v1: 0.7917/0.7292); single-pass arm of H349 re-baselines on R30-tuned feeding. Order: R30 (knee + document_concurrency) -> H351 (immediate, pure replay) -> H353 (chunk-level gate) -> H349/H350 full ingests ONLY on gate pass -> H352. Defects: H349/H351 close DEF-11, H349+H352 close DEF-13, R30 closes DEF-12.

## R32 - chunking round: the window is a quality decision, not plumbing (user-directed, pre-registered 2026-07-10)

User directive 2026-07-10: creative fanout on chunking strategy, survey-first. A web survey (research agent, Opus, 2026-07-10) grounded the round; every cited paper is archived under `references/papers/` per the reference rule. The survey's two decisive facts: (1) nearly all published chunking evidence is RETRIEVAL-side - the only extraction-side numbers are the chunk-size ablations: GraphRAG extracted ~2x the entity references at 600-token chunks vs 2400 (gleaning recovers large-window recall), and LightRAG's entity/relationship counts rise monotonically as chunks shrink 2000 -> 500; KGF ships 2000-token windows, squarely the published low-yield regime. (2) No chunking policy can create co-occurrence that does not exist inside a window - cross-section relations (DocRED: 40.7% of relational facts are multi-sentence-only) need a document-level relation pass, not a smarter splitter. Pre-registered dead ends, excluded with evidence: embedding-drift semantic chunking (fixed-size matched or beat it retrieval-side - HotpotQA F1@5 90.59 fixed vs 87.37 breakpoint vs 84.79 clustering - and zero extraction-side evidence exists; [`[paper digest] is semantic chunking worth the cost.md`](../../references/papers/)), late chunking (changes the retrieval vector, never the text the extractor reads), agentic/LLM-grouping chunking (no benchmark evidence, highest cost). R07-H36 (whole-doc extraction, pending since R07) is SUPERSEDED by this round: H355 and H361 decompose its claim into gated, cheaper forms. SEQUENCING: the round executes AFTER the R31 verdicts - chunking effects are confounded with emission variance until union-of-K is adjudicated; sole exception H354 (offline census, no GPU, no confound), which gates everything else. Failure-class taxonomy, measured by H354: (i) in-chunk emission miss (R31's territory, NOT chunking), (ii) seam-straddling, (iii) no co-occurrence window for relation endpoints, (iv) subject-sparsity (the H21 zero-chunk class), (v) table-severed.

### R32-H354 CENSUS GATE - weigh the chunking failure classes before changing any splitter

- **Persona** - contrarian (says most residual failure mass may not be boundary-attributable at all - the round can die here for free)
- **Grounding** - R22-H231 attributes the dominant loss to WITHIN-chunk emission undersampling (single pass 55.4% gold-carrier coverage), which no splitter touches; the H21 iteration-4 forensic found a manual's subject in ZERO chunks of its own document (class iv exists); H153 measured table severance (class v exists); the 200-token overlap already defends class (ii); nobody has weighed the classes against each other
- **Hypothesis** - an offline census decomposes each failed probe and each gold string into classes (i)-(v) by pure text analysis over the shipped chunk pool; class masses gate H355-H361 individually
- **Prediction** - class (i) carries >= 50% of residual failure mass (consistent with H231); class (ii) <= 15% (overlap covers most seams); classes (iii)+(iv) carry the probe-relevant remainder
- **Acceptance bar** - GATE for the round: each downstream hypothesis runs only if its target class carries >= 10% of post-R31 residual failure mass; a class under 10% kills its hypothesis pre-build
- **Experiment** - offline, no GPU, runs any time (does not wait for R31): shipped chunk pool (`chunking.py`, 2000/200), 24-probe gold evidence + freshest failed-probe stats, h158 `_present` matcher; census script checked into `scripts/`
- **Result (main session 2026-07-10)** - ([`r32-h354-census-20260710T125440Z.json`](../../reports/r32-h354-census-20260710T125440Z.json), `scripts/r32_h354_census.py`; failed set = 7 probes / mass 5.0 from the freshest per-probe recall map `h212-v2-recall-rerun.json`, emission evidence = the H353 6-pass record) the contrarian persona was right beyond its own prediction - NONE of the residual failure mass is boundary-attributable: **60% `i_emission_covered`** (golds with carriers, emitted in 6/6 H353 passes - P09/P11/P16/P17 dimension/weight/ramp specs - the failure is DOWNSTREAM of extraction: resolution, graph shape, or retrieval ranking), **20% `parse_loss_reader`** (P08 "Typical power consumption: 9.0W" absent from every extracted document text - the READER never produced it, upstream of chunking), **20% `iii_cross_document`** (P15/P18 comparison probes whose two golds live in different PDFs - '1.98kg' only in the DreamStation doc, '2.4 kg' only in BMC/other docs - no splitter and no per-document relation pass can co-window across documents). Classes (i-miss), (ii), (iv), (v) all measured at ZERO mass. Caveats: recall map from the v2-h212 graph vs emissions measured now (instrument mix, directionally robust); 24-probe set is small; classes weighed on probe-linked golds only
- **Verdict** - **CENSUS GATE KILLS THE ENTIRE ROUND: H355, H356, H357, H358, H359, H360, H361 all at 0% target-class mass vs the 10% bar - closed pre-build, zero builds and zero GPU spent.** The DocRED/GraphRAG-derived expectations do not transfer to this corpus + probe set. Standing consequence: the residual failure mechanism is NOT chunking and (per H353) NOT sampling - the dominant class (60%) is emitted-but-lost, contradicting H107's never-extracted theory on the current engine; localization registered as H364 below. The parse-loss and cross-document classes get their fix hypotheses only after H364 locates the loss stage (evidence-first)
- **Addendum (2026-07-10, post-H364 drift discovery)** - the census's recall map was measured on a graph state that no longer exists: neo4j4 is a layered experiment pile (h212-v2 rerun 1094 ents/3946 rels -> +H241 v1/v2 -> +H240b enum/mention -> live 5213/23472), exposed when H364's first walk returned 50% `recovered_now`. Fresh consistent pair measured on the live graph ([`h364-fresh-recall-20260710T135437Z.json`](../../reports/h364-fresh-recall-20260710T135437Z.json): mean 0.7917, failed set MOVED to P08/P09/P15/P16/P21/P22 - P11/P17/P18 now pass, P21/P22 regressed 1.0 -> 0.0) and the census re-derived against it ([`r32-h354-census-20260710T135843Z.json`](../../reports/r32-h354-census-20260710T135843Z.json)): `i_emission_covered` 30%, `parse_loss_reader` 20%, `i_emission_deterministic` 20% (P21 'gradually acclimate'), `i_emission_stochastic` 20% (P22 'amplitude of oscillations'), `iii_cross_document` 10%. **H355 REVIVES at 40%** (the i-miss classes clear the 10% bar on the fresh map) - forward-valid because the pinned recipe never/rarely emits the P21/P22 golds (H353 record) and both strings are verified absent from every entity surface on the live graph, so a clean rebuild fails those probes too; the kill of H356-H361 stands. P21/P22's regression (renderable on the v2 graph, zero carriers now, while union ingest only ADDS) additionally evidences description erosion through union-layer resolution merges - a resolution-stage effect, folded into H364's evidence

- **Persona** - conformist (the only chunking lever with extraction-side published numbers)
- **Grounding** - GraphRAG: ~2x entity references at 600-token vs 2400-token chunks, gleaning recovers large-window recall ([`[paper digest] Microsoft GraphRAG.md`](../../references/papers/)); LightRAG: entity/relation counts rise monotonically 2000 -> 500 ([`[paper digest] LightRAG.md`](../../references/papers/)); KGF ships 2000 + gleaning_rounds=1; the extra calls smaller chunks cost are paid by the idle card (DEF-12/R30 economy, same as union-of-K); known trade: smaller chunks raise recall and lower precision - identity clauses guard
- **Hypothesis** - re-chunking at 600-1200 raises per-pass gold emission coverage; composes with R31's union verdict (both are emission samplers)
- **Prediction** - chunk-gate (H353 instrument on re-chunked pools): single-pass gold emission at 600 tokens >= +10 points over the 2000-token baseline (~0.55)
- **Acceptance bar** - chunk-gate PASS if >= +10 emission points at 600 or 1200; then ONE full ingest at the winning size: never-extracted census <= baseline, identity clauses hold (H349 form), wall-clock <= 2x the R30-tuned baseline; N>=3 runs per the H351 form before any default flip
- **Experiment** - re-chunk at 600/1200 (`chunk_size` knob exists), 6 passes over gold-carrier chunks at the R30 knee, H353 instrument; ingest only on gate pass, post-R31; source: GraphRAG + LightRAG digests + originals (`references/papers/`)

### R32-H356 Cross-section relations need a document-level relation pass - no splitter creates co-occurrence

- **Persona** - heretical (says the failure class chunking methods claim to fix is structurally unfixable by chunking)
- **Grounding** - DocRED: 40.7% of relational facts are multi-sentence-only ([`[paper digest] docred document-level relation extraction.md`](../../references/papers/)); class (iii) = endpoint pairs never sharing a chunk; mention detection is already per-chunk and name-strong (H246 enumerate); `relation_only_messages` exists (`prompts.py`) - the second stage is a prompt over pooled mentions, not a trained model
- **Hypothesis** - two-stage: per-chunk extraction collects mentions (unchanged); a document-level pass over the pooled mention list (names + types + section paths, compact - not full text) infers relations for pairs that never co-occurred
- **Prediction** - recovers >= 50% of class-(iii) gold relations at precision >= the within-chunk relation baseline
- **Acceptance bar** - GATED on H354 class (iii) >= 10%; PASS if >= half the class-(iii) golds land AND precision >= the within-chunk baseline; REFUTED if the pass hallucinates (precision < baseline - 0.1)
- **Experiment** - post-R31; pooled mention list from a fresh ingest's graph, per-document relation pass on the local model; source: DocRED digest + original

### R32-H357 Deterministic subject + breadcrumb injection - every chunk names its device and section

- **Persona** - conformist (cheapest lever in the round; the H21 forensic already proved the mechanism's absence)
- **Grounding** - H21 iteration-4 forensic: 'SleepStyle 200 Series' appears in ZERO chunks of its own manual - pages of "the device"; subject and section path are string operations over the parser's own markdown heading tree (the filename-token candidacy machinery exists, `aliases.py`); the LLM-generated escalation of the same idea is contextual retrieval (vendor-measured -35/-49/-67% retrieval failures, retrieval-side; mechanism reference https://www.anthropic.com/engineering/contextual-retrieval)
- **Hypothesis** - prepending `subject > section path` to each chunk's text (deterministic, zero LLM cost) anchors extraction; class (iv) shrinks
- **Prediction** - chunk-gate on class-(iv) carriers: emission +15 points or better with injection; global emission non-regressing
- **Acceptance bar** - GATED on class (iv) >= 10%; chunk-gate PASS -> wire behind a config flag + verification ingest post-R31; REFUTED if injection produces wrong-subject over-attribution (the H22-iter-3 class, watched via the fidelity probes)
- **Experiment** - string-op prototype on chunking output; H353-instrument A/B (injected vs bare) on class-(iv) carrier chunks

### R32-H358 Coreference continuity - the LLM escalation where static injection leaves residue

- **Persona** - follower (conditional escalation of H357, runs only on its residue)
- **Grounding** - LINK-KG: coref-resolved LLM KG pipelines yield more complete, less fragmented graphs; even sparse pronoun references materially affect completeness (extraction-side evidence, other domains; [`[paper digest] link-kg coref kg construction.md`](../../references/papers/)); manuals name the device once then say "the unit/it" for pages
- **Hypothesis** - an LLM rewrite resolving deictic/pronoun references to canonical subject names on the chunks H357's static injection did not recover recovers >= half of that residue
- **Prediction** - >= 50% of the H357 residue recovers; no fidelity regression
- **Acceptance bar** - conditional: runs ONLY if H357 passes its gate but leaves >= 10 gold strings unrecovered in class (iv); PASS if >= 50% residue recovery at zero fidelity regression; else class (iv) closes as extraction-hard
- **Experiment** - local-model rewrite pass over the residue chunks (idle-card economics), same instrument; source: LINK-KG digest + original

### R32-H359 SaT boundaries replace regex snapping - seam quality at zero GPU cost

- **Persona** - conformist (a strictly better boundary primitive; the open question is only whether the seam class carries mass)
- **Grounding** - SaT (EMNLP 2024): F1 84.9-93.1 over 81-85 languages, the margin widening on noisy text (81.8 vs 58.2 macro on corrupted pairs) - exactly the PDF-extracted-text regime; CPU inference ~1,000 sentences/0.5 s; the shipped snap is a 3-pattern regex ([`[paper digest] segment any text sat.md`](../../references/papers/))
- **Hypothesis** - SaT cut points (sentence + paragraph mode) at identical chunk_size/overlap reduce mid-entity/mid-fact seam cuts vs the regex snap
- **Prediction** - seam-straddle census (H354 instrument) halves on the re-chunked pool; gold emission non-regressing
- **Acceptance bar** - GATED on class (ii) >= 10%; PASS if the seam census <= half AND emission non-regressing; ships as the default snapper (CPU-only, zero GPU cost)
- **Experiment** - `wtpsplit` sat-3l-sm on CPU, re-chunk, census diff + 6-pass emission check on carrier chunks; source: SaT digest + original

### R32-H360 Tables are atomic units - the H153 patch covers the symptom, not the unit decision

- **Persona** - contrarian (says H153's header re-print patches a wrong unit decision - the table should never be severed)
- **Grounding** - H153 shipped (0.258% overhead, zero false injections) but re-prints headers only; severed tables still divorce row groups from section context; the enumeration-dense catalog class (H240b, H352) is table/list-dominated; `_find_tables` already detects markdown tables (`chunking.py`)
- **Hypothesis** - chunking that treats a detected table + its section header as an atomic unit (splitting only BETWEEN row groups, header re-printed, when a table alone exceeds the budget) lifts emission on table-carried golds beyond the H153 treatment
- **Prediction** - class-(v) emission +10 points or better; cleaner enumeration boundaries assist H352's budget
- **Acceptance bar** - GATED on class (v) >= 10%; chunk-gate PASS -> wire behind flag; REFUTED if atomic tables force worse cuts elsewhere (global emission regression)
- **Experiment** - native extension of `_find_tables` (docling evaluated only if native falls short); H353-instrument A/B on table-carried golds

### R32-H361 Section-scoped extraction - H36's heir, bounded by lost-in-the-middle

- **Persona** - heretical (chunking is a quality decision and the honest unit is the SECTION, not the token window)
- **Grounding** - R07-H36 (pending since R07) predicted whole-doc extraction recovers boundary losses but was never run; lost-in-the-middle caps the form: >30% accuracy drop mid-context across models ([`[paper digest] Lost in the Middle.md`](../../references/papers/)), and GraphRAG's ablation shows large windows extract FEWER entities per unit text - whole-DOC is the wrong unit; whole-SECTION (heading tree, typically 1-6k tokens) keeps related facts in one window under the attention/yield cliff; class (iii) decomposes into same-section vs truly-cross-section pairs - H361 targets the former, H356 the latter
- **Hypothesis** - section-scoped windows (split at heading boundaries only; oversized sections sub-split) recover same-section class-(iii) relations that 2000-token windows sever
- **Prediction** - census shows >= half of class-(iii) pairs share a section; section-scoped extraction recovers >= half of those at within-chunk precision; per-entity yield holds (the GraphRAG yield guardrail)
- **Acceptance bar** - GATED on the same-section share of class (iii) >= 10% of failure mass; PASS if recovery >= half at baseline precision AND entity yield non-regressing; H36 closes as SUPERSEDED by this hypothesis regardless of outcome
- **Experiment** - heading-tree section splitter; A/B on same-section class-(iii) pairs; post-R31; source: Lost in the Middle digest + original
