Baseline: shipped chunking (2000/200, regex snap, H153 carryover) on the post-R31 engine at R30-tuned feeding; all chunk-gates reuse the H353 instrument (6 passes over carrier chunks at the measured knee). Order: H354 immediately (offline) -> chunk-gates for surviving hypotheses (H355/H357/H359/H360, idle-card) -> ingest-level verdicts post-R31 -> H356/H361 relation recovery -> H358 conditional. The fanout is 8, not 10: embedding-drift semantic chunking and late chunking were deliberately not registered (dead ends per the preamble, evidence archived). Defects: the round targets DEF-13's post-R31 residue.

**Round closure (2026-07-10)**: H354's census killed every downstream hypothesis pre-build (all target classes at 0% of residual mass vs the 10% bar) - the round closes with one offline script spent and zero GPU. The residue's real mechanisms route to R33.

## R33 - failure-stage localization: the residue is emitted but lost (registered 2026-07-10, standing authorization)

The H353 gate and H354 census jointly overturned the standing failure theory: extraction emits 60% of the residual failure mass reliably (6/6 passes at chunk level), yet the probes still fail at recall@16 - so the loss sits BETWEEN emission and retrieval, in resolution, graph shape, or ranking. This contradicts H107's never-extracted forensics on the current engine. Two further classes route elsewhere: reader parse loss (20%, "9.0W" never extracted from the PDF) and cross-document comparison probes (20%, golds in different documents - a retrieval/ranking property). Fix hypotheses register only AFTER H364 locates the stage (evidence-first; no fixing a stage we have not measured).

### R33-H364 Localization census - walk each emitted-but-lost gold through the pipeline stages on the freshest graph

- **Persona** - contrarian (the fix everyone would reach for - retrieval tuning - may be the wrong stage; the census refuses to guess)
- **Grounding** - H354: P09/P11/P16/P17 golds (dimension/weight/ramp specs) carried + emitted 6/6, probes still failing; H107 (older engine) found the opposite (absent from graph); the two can only be reconciled by checking the CURRENT graph stage by stage; near-deterministic instruments preferred (H351 lesson)
- **Hypothesis** - for each emitted-but-lost gold, a stage walk over the freshest rebuilt graph - (a) is the gold string present on any graph node/property (loader survived)? (b) if present, was it merged/demoted into an entity whose name no longer matches (resolution loss)? (c) if present and distinct, does it appear in top-k retrieval for its probe (ranking loss)? (d) if retrieved, does the render surface it (render gap)? - assigns each gold exactly one loss stage
- **Prediction** - the mass splits between resolution loss (merged into spec-soup entities) and ranking loss (present but below top-16); loader loss ~0
- **Acceptance bar** - every emitted-but-lost gold assigned exactly one stage with graph-query evidence; a stage carrying >= 50% of the walked mass becomes the registered fix target for R33's follow-on hypotheses; if no stage dominates, per-class fixes register individually. Naive baseline: none (pure diagnosis)
- **Experiment** - offline + Neo4j queries against the freshest complete graph (no GPU; runs during the H363 ramp); script checked into `scripts/`; outputs per-gold stage table + stage masses
- **Result (main session 2026-07-10)** - (`scripts/r33_h364_stage_census.py`; stale-map walk [`r33-h364-stage-census-20260710T135223Z.json`](../../reports/r33-h364-stage-census-20260710T135223Z.json) exposed graph drift - see H354 addendum; fresh consistent pair: recall [`h364-fresh-recall-20260710T135437Z.json`](../../reports/h364-fresh-recall-20260710T135437Z.json) + census [`r32-h354-census-20260710T135843Z.json`](../../reports/r32-h354-census-20260710T135843Z.json) + walk [`r33-h364-stage-census-20260710T135954Z.json`](../../reports/r33-h364-stage-census-20260710T135954Z.json)) fresh emitted-but-lost class = the P09/P16 dimension golds, walked with the harness's own primitives (Titan embedding -> vector top-200 -> `_render_nodes` -> `_present`): **`resolution_loss` 1.25 of 1.5 walked mass (83.3%)**, `recovered_now` 0.25 (equal-split artifact: P16's iBreeze gold '238*178*128 mm' is found at rank 2). Evidence: '275mm x 170mm x 140mm' (SleepStyle 200 dims) sits ONLY in `prop_*` of the seven HC-model-code entities (HC231/232/233/234/236/238 DeviceModel + 'HC230 Product Range' DeviceSeries); the probe's vector search retrieves the RIGHT subjects ('Fisher & Paykel SleepStyle', 'HC230-Series', 'Sleep Style 200 Series' at ranks 1-3) but all three carry ZERO `prop_*` and have NO relationship to any carrier; the carriers sit at rank 133+/absent-from-top-200 with zero subject-token overlap; the children link `PART_OF -> 'HC230 Product Range'` - an unmerged duplicate of the retrieved 'HC230-Series'. Two compounding mechanisms: (1) same-entity fragmentation the resolver never merged ('HC230-Series' retrieved-but-empty vs 'HC230 Product Range' carrying-but-unreachable, zero cross-links); (2) the render is name-only at 1 hop, so a linked child's `prop_*` can never surface - the carrier itself must enter top-16
- **Verdict** - **CONFIRMED (prediction half-right): resolution loss carries 83.3% of walked mass - over the 50% bar, RESOLUTION IS THE REGISTERED FIX TARGET; ranking loss measured at ZERO** (retrieval finds the correct subjects - they are simply empty and disconnected). Loader loss 0 as predicted. Bar met: every walked gold assigned exactly one stage with graph-query evidence. Fix registered as H365; P08 parse-loss and P15 cross-document classes stay parked pending H365

### R33-H365 Spec reachability repair - merge the series fragments and hoist child-model specs to the retrieved subject

- **Persona** - conformist (the direct fix for the measured mechanism: the specs exist, the subjects are retrieved, only the attachment path is broken)
- **Grounding** - H364: dims only on HC23x children (rank 133+/absent), children link `PART_OF -> 'HC230 Product Range'` which duplicates the retrieved 'HC230-Series' (zero cross-links, all retrieved subjects property-less); name-only 1-hop render means a fragment merge ALONE cannot surface child props - the retrievable entity must carry them; retrieval-first principle (perfect context in 1-2 hops, work shifts to ingest time)
- **Hypothesis** - a resolution-stage reachability repair - (a) merge duplicate series fragments, (b) hoist/mirror spec-class `prop_*` from child models onto the merged, retrievable series entity (provenance-tagged, no overwrite of conflicting values) - flips the emitted-but-lost dimension golds into top-16 render
- **Prediction** - P09 0.0 -> 1.0 and P16 0.5 -> 1.0 on the repaired graph; the other 22 probes non-regressing
- **Acceptance bar** - offline prototype first (Cypher/Python on the live graph, no GPU): P09/P16 `_present` true in top-16 render AND zero regressions across the remaining probes; only then the same logic registers as an engine resolution/load change, verified on the goal-terminal clean rebuild; REFUTED if hoisting produces spec-soup on the parent (conflicting child specs colliding - watched via the fidelity probes)
- **Experiment** - offline repair pass on neo4j4 + fresh recall@16 re-measure (h158 harness); engine wiring gated on the prototype flipping the probes; runs during/after H363
- **Result (main session 2026-07-10)** - (`scripts/r33_h365_repair.py`, applied log `logs/r33-h365-repair.log`; verdict measure [`h365-repaired-recall-20260710T143152Z.json`](../../reports/)) all 12 child spec props measured UNANIMOUS across HC231-238 (including `prop_dimensions` = the gold) - the spec-soup failure mode structurally could not fire; hoisted 12 props each onto 'HC230-Series' and 'Sleep Style 200 Series' (provenance marker `h365_hoisted`, no overwrites) + 6 additive `PART_OF {h365:true}` edges as the merge simulation. Recall@16 re-measure: **P09 0.0 -> 1.0, P16 0.5 -> 1.0, the other 22 probes bit-identical to baseline - zero regressions; mean 0.7917 -> 0.8542, fully covered 18 -> 20**
- **Verdict** - **CONFIRMED at prototype stage: prediction hit exactly (both probes flipped, 22 flat).** The H364 stage assignment is now causally validated - a resolution-stage repair alone recovered the walked mass, no retrieval or render change needed. Remaining per the bar: register the same logic as an engine resolution/load change (series-fragment merge + unanimous-spec hoist) and verify it on the goal-terminal clean rebuild; the prototype's additive marks (`h365_hoisted`, `PART_OF {h365:true}`) are one-shot reversible on neo4j4
- **Engine wiring (2026-07-10 night)** - generalized corpus-agnostically in `graph/hoist.py` under the `resolution.spec_hoist` lever (default off until the clean-rebuild verification): `bridge_series_fragments` treats entities sharing a model-code stem whose names BOTH carry a series marker (series/range/family/line) as fragments of one series and PART_OF-bridges the union of their children to every fragment (`spec_bridge=true` edges); `hoist_unanimous_specs` copies `prop_*` keys carried by EVERY child with one identical value onto series-marker parents only - the marker guard makes component-to-product hoists (battery specs onto a device) structurally impossible, and no-overwrite plus the accumulating `spec_hoisted` key list make the pass exactly reversible. Runs at `optimize()` BEFORE proposition generation (propositions derive from `prop_*`, so hoisted specs materialize as propositions on the series entities). The pile's fragment pair validates the stem rule ('HC230-Series'/'HC230 Product Range' -> HC230; cosine between them is only 0.7477, below every resolver threshold - embedding similarity could never find this merge); 'Sleep Style 200 Series' has no shared stem and is reachable only if the rebuild's extraction links children to it (the clean-rebuild verification will show). 428 unit + 10 live integration tests green, including an exact-rollback integration test (markers restore the graph bit-identically; dev instance verified zero residue)

| Hypothesis | Persona | Lever | Pre-reg prediction | Acceptance bar |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| R33-H364 | contrarian | per-gold pipeline stage walk | resolution + ranking split the mass; loader ~0 | every gold assigned one stage with evidence; >= 50% stage becomes the fix target |
| R33-H365 | conformist | fragment merge + spec hoist at resolution | P09 1.0, P16 1.0, 22 probes flat | prototype flips probes with zero regressions; engine wiring only after |

Defects: DEF-13 (the 60% emitted-but-lost class + 20% parse loss), DEF-11 secondarily (naming churn measured at the resolution stage). The parse-loss gold ("9.0W") and the cross-document class get fix hypotheses after H364's verdict.

## R34 - HippoRAG mechanism transfer: re-activate the dormant substrate (registered 2026-07-10, user directive, research by 2 primed agents)

Two research passes (mechanism mining over the archived HippoRAG-1/2 PDFs; a file:line capability map of KGF's retrieval path) establish that KGF already CONTAINS most of HippoRAG's winning substrate in dormant or disconnected form. Peer lever sizes from the papers' own ablations (multi-hop passage recall@5 unless noted): PPR +13.7 to +16.7 (H1 Table 5), query-to-triple linking +12.5 (H2 Table 4, +20.9 on MuSiQue), passage nodes in the graph +6.1 (H2, +11.0 MuSiQue), synonym edges +2.4 (H1), node specificity +2.0 (H1), recognition-memory LLM filter only +0.7 and the paper's best-documented failure surface (26% of failure samples lose all supporting phrases to the filter) - **the recognition filter is explicitly NOT registered** (weakest lever, highest per-query cost, worst failure profile). KGF state (capability map, 2026-07-10): Chunk nodes stored but never embedded and never rendered (`pipeline.py:621-640`, chunks are PPR conduits only); proposition index EXISTS with query-to-triple matching but its entity seeds feed only the dormant PPR (`pipeline.py:1061-1083`); PPR implemented on GDS but `ppr_enabled=False` (H37 refutation: 0.994 containment in seeds+1hop - measured on the 28-doc CPAP graph); SIMILAR_TO edges created three ways at ingest then excluded from render (`pipeline.py:1157,1168`) and consumed by nothing; no specificity weighting. Naive baseline for the round: the default composition on the post-H365 live graph - recall@16 = 0.8542, failed set P08/P15/P21/P22 (mass 3.5). Why GraphRAG/LightRAG lose these benches (for the record): they answer from LLM-generated summary artifacts instead of ranking source evidence - GraphRAG avg F1 49.6 vs HippoRAG-2 59.8 at 12.5x the indexing tokens; LightRAG collapses to avg F1 6.6. KGF's render is closer to their losing shape than to HippoRAG's evidence-ranking shape - R34+R35 move it toward evidence.

### R34-H366 Passage nodes as retrieval targets - verbatim source text joins the vector pool

- **Persona** - conformist (the largest cheap peer lever, and it converges with H364's render-gap finding and the P21/P22 description-erosion evidence from the opposite direction)
- **Grounding** - HippoRAG-2 +6.1 avg / +11.0 MuSiQue recall@5 from passage nodes ([`[paper digest] HippoRAG 2.md`](../../references/papers/)); KGF stores `(:Chunk {text})` with MENTIONED_IN edges but never embeds nor renders them; P21 'gradually acclimate' and P22 'amplitude of oscillations' are VERBATIM in carrier chunks (H354 census) yet absent from every entity surface (H364 check) - verbatim passage retrieval is immune to merge-time description erosion; competes with revived H355 for the same 40% emission-residue mass at far lower cost (no re-chunking, no re-ingest)
- **Hypothesis** - embedding chunk texts (Titan, same pool or parallel index queried jointly) and rendering top-scoring chunk text into context recovers the emission-residue probes without touching extraction
- **Prediction** - P21 0.0 -> 1.0, P22 0.0 -> 1.0 on the live graph; mean recall +0.08 or better; P08 stays 0.0 (gold never parsed - passage nodes cannot carry it)
- **Acceptance bar** - offline prototype (embed 247 chunks, extend harness render with a chunks section, re-measure recall@16): both probes flip, zero regressions on the other 22; context token growth <= 30%; engine wiring + clean-rebuild verification only after the prototype passes (H365 discipline)
- **Experiment** - offline, no GPU (Bedrock embeddings), runs during H363; sources: HippoRAG 2 digest + PDF Table 4
- **Note (2026-07-10, instrument change pre-run)** - user directive: no Titan for new bulk embedding compute; the passage channel embeds LOCALLY on GPU (BAAI/bge-m3, 1024-dim, 8192-token context, fp16 on the RTX 5000 Ada, idx 2) with the models configurable per the new acc-crit Embeddings section; the ENTITY channel keeps the harness's Titan query embedding because the live entity index is Titan-space - the control arm stays fixed. Script `scripts/r34_h366_passages.py`, cache `results/r34/h366-chunk-embs-bgem3.jsonl`. Registered predictions and bar unchanged
- **Result** - three arms on the post-H365 pile (5213 ents), 247-chunk pool: (1) full-chunk M-sweep ([`r34-h366-passages-20260710T154339Z.json`](../../reports/r34-h366-passages-20260710T154339Z.json)): M=0 reproduced the 0.8542 baseline EXACTLY; M=1 mean 0.9583, fully covered 23/24 - P21 0.0 -> 1.0, P22 0.0 -> 1.0, P15 0.5 -> 1.0 bonus, 21 probes flat, zero regressions, P08 0.0 as predicted - but context +78% (bar breach); M=2/M=4 add nothing (identical recall at +160%/+321%). (2) head-truncation arm ([`r34-h366-trunc-20260710T160845Z.json`](../../reports/r34-h366-trunc-20260710T160845Z.json)): structurally lossy - the golds sit deep (P21 in (800,1200], P22 and P15's second gold > 1200 chars into their chunks); T1200 keeps only P21 at +12.8%. (3) query-anchored window arm ([`r34-h366-window-20260710T161609Z.json`](../../reports/r34-h366-window-20260710T161609Z.json), `scripts/r34_h366_window_arm.py` - sliding spans embedded in the same bge-m3 space, top-k spans by query similarity): ALL four configs (span 900/1200 x k 1/2) hit 0.9375, 22/24, P21+P22 flipped, zero regressions - the cheapest (span 900, k=1) at +9.5% growth, well inside the 30% budget. P15 stays 0.5 in window arms (its golds sit in different documents - only the full-chunk fluke of a comparison chunk covered both; this is H372's registered class)
- **Verdict** - **CONFIRMED (2026-07-10)** - the registered bar passes at the span-900/k-1 operating point: P21+P22 flip, zero regressions on the other 22, +9.5% context growth (<= 30%); mean recall 0.8542 -> 0.9375 with no extraction change. Engine-wiring consequence: render query-anchored SPANS, never chunk heads (truncation refuted) and never whole chunks (budget). Overlap caveat (DEF-14, discovered by H369's base arm): the shipped engine's `overfetch_factor=4` read path - which the pinned harness omits - recovers P21 by itself (parity baseline 0.8958); the P22 flip and the lift beyond 0.8958 are the passage channel's own. Wiring lands with the R34 engine tranche; the passage index rides the acc-crit Embeddings provider abstraction (local bge-m3 space, per-channel provenance)

### R34-H367 Proposition-seeded entities enter the render - the +12.5 lever is 80 percent built and disconnected

- **Persona** - conformist (query-to-triple is HippoRAG-2's largest ablated lever; KGF has the index, the matcher, and the seeds - they just route to a disabled consumer)
- **Grounding** - H2 query-to-triple +12.5 avg recall@5 (+20.9 MuSiQue) vs entity-name seeding; KGF's `proposition_query` already matches queries to S-P-O sentences (top_k=8, diversity-filtered) and `proposition_seeding=True` already extends the seed-id set - but only PPR consumes the extension and PPR is off, so proposition hits contribute Facts text without their entities' full blocks (`pipeline.py:1061-1083`)
- **Hypothesis** - routing proposition-hit ABOUT entities into the rendered seed set (union with vector seeds under the same top_k budget) lifts spec-class recall - the fact's OWNER entity gets its properties and relations rendered, not just the one matched sentence
- **Prediction** - net positive on the failed set or flat with reduced rank-position variance; no regression (the Facts section already carries the sentences - this adds the blocks)
- **Acceptance bar** - offline harness A/B (extend `_retrieve_local` behind a config flag): zero regressions, any failed-probe gain counts; REFUTED if seed dilution pushes gold entities out of the render budget (measure fully_covered alongside)
- **Experiment** - offline, config-flagged, harness re-measure on the live graph; source: HippoRAG 2 digest Section 3.3
- **Result** - ([`r34-h367-propseeds-20260710T161901Z.json`](../../reports/r34-h367-propseeds-20260710T161901Z.json), `scripts/r34_h367_prop_seeds.py`; 11,698 deterministic propositions rebuilt in memory from the live pile, embedded locally in bge-m3 space per the embeddings directive - the proposition channel never existed in Titan space, so query and index sides are consistently local): arm A (baseline) reproduced 0.8542/20 exactly; arm B (budget union - top-8 diversified proposition hits' ABOUT entities displace the vector tail, 16 total) **P15 0.5 -> 1.0, zero regressions, 0.8750/21 at +19% context**; arm C (additive, budget exceeded) identical recall at +66% - B dominates; arm D (fact sentences appended, no entity blocks) DEAD FLAT 0.8542 - the sentences alone carry nothing, the OWNER entities' rendered blocks carry everything, exactly the registered mechanism
- **Verdict** - **CONFIRMED (2026-07-10)** - the registered bar passes at arm B: a failed-probe gain (P15, the cross-document comparison probe) with zero regressions and no budget flood (fully_covered 20 -> 21). The seed-dilution refutation mode did not fire even with 4 displaced vector seeds. Two consequences: (1) P15 was H372's predicted target - the free deterministic proposition channel reaches the cross-document class because propositions are generated over MERGED entity evidence, so H372's bar must now show a gain beyond what H367 already delivers (recorded before H372 runs); (2) wiring is one line of routing - `_retrieve_local` already computes the union into `seed_ids`, it just needs `nodes` to consume it when PPR is off (pipeline.py:1085)

### R34-H368 PPR re-ablation at benchmark scale - the H37 refutation is a small-graph property

- **Persona** - contrarian to our own verdict (H37 called PPR theater at 0.994 seed+1hop containment - on 28 documents; HippoRAG measures +13.7 to +16.7 on 10k-passage pools where hop chains actually span the graph; supersede-with-back-reference discipline: the H37 verdict STANDS for CPAP scale)
- **Grounding** - H1 Table 5: removing PPR costs -16.7 avg recall@5; the containment argument mathematically weakens as graph diameter grows; KGF's GDS implementation is retained for ablation (`settings.py:127`)
- **Hypothesis** - on the first benchmark-corpus build (HotpotQA-class pool), PPR over the H366 passage+entity graph adds recall the 1-hop render cannot reach
- **Prediction** - seed+1hop containment drops below 0.9 at 10k-passage scale; PPR-on beats PPR-off on passage recall@5 by >= 3 points
- **Acceptance bar** - GATED on the benchmark harness build (task #59): A/B on the smoke slice first; PPR promotes only if recall gain >= 3 points at <= 1.5x query latency; else H37 generalizes and PPR retires permanently
- **Experiment** - rides the benchmark campaign's first build; zero cost until then; sources: HippoRAG digest + H37 verdict back-reference

### R34-H369 Node specificity weighting - the free IDF prior

- **Persona** - conformist (free at query time, +2.0 peer, zero risk profile)
- **Grounding** - H1 node specificity s_i = |sources|^-1 scales reset probability: +2.0 avg, +4.0 HotpotQA; KGF ranks purely by vector score - over-connected generic entities (H364 walk: 'iBreeze CPAP System' carries 324 rels and matched a 2819-carrier gold artifact) crowd seed lists
- **Hypothesis** - down-weighting seed scores by source-chunk count (s_i = 1/|source_chunks|, log-damped) sharpens seed lists toward specific entities
- **Prediction** - flat-to-positive mean recall; reduced generic-entity occupancy in top-16 (measurable: mean seed degree drops)
- **Acceptance bar** - offline harness A/B behind a config knob: zero regressions required, any gain counts; REFUTED if specificity demotes legitimate hub answers (probes about series-level entities regress)
- **Experiment** - offline, config-flagged, live-graph re-measure; source: HippoRAG digest Section 2.3
- **Result** - ([`r34-h369-specificity-20260710T161534Z.json`](../../reports/r34-h369-specificity-20260710T161534Z.json), `scripts/r34_h369_specificity.py`; overfetch-64 pool, rerank by score x s_i, truncate to 16) both variants CRATER: linear and log identically 0.4792 mean, 9/24 fully covered vs base 0.8958/21 - 13 of 24 probes regressed. Mechanism visible in the occupancy metrics: mean seed source_chunks collapses 3.95 -> 1.0 and mean degree 26.8 -> 5.1 - the multiplicative prior hands the entire top-16 to single-chunk entities, evicting every legitimately multi-sourced answer (series-level and hub entities). The registered refutation clause fired exactly. Side discovery: the base arm (overfetch-64, head-16) scores 0.8958 vs the pinned harness's direct-16 0.8542, single differing probe P21 - the shipped engine's `overfetch_factor=4` (R15-H195a) was never mirrored into the harness; filed as DEF-14 (instrument parity)
- **Verdict** - **REFUTED (2026-07-10)** - as a hard seed-rerank transplant, specificity weighting is dead: HippoRAG applies s_i as a SOFT scale on PPR reset probability, not as a rank multiplier; the hard transplant is 10x more aggressive than the peer mechanism and destroys multi-source answers. Any revival belongs inside H368's PPR re-ablation at benchmark scale (where the soft form is native), not as a seed filter. The lasting yield is DEF-14

### R34-H370 Synonym-edge traversal - three creators, zero consumers

- **Persona** - contrarian (KGF pays for SIMILAR_TO at ingest - kNN densification, defer-zone soft links, demotion court - then excludes them from every read path; either they earn render access or their creation should be questioned)
- **Grounding** - H1 synonym edges +2.4 avg recall@5 (80% of H2's edge mass); KGF render excludes `SIMILAR_TO` explicitly (`pipeline.py:1157`) and the soft-link posterior `weight` is consumed by NOTHING (capability map find); the H364 fragment pair ('HC230-Series' / 'HC230 Product Range') is exactly the class a similarity hop would bridge without waiting for a merge verdict
- **Hypothesis** - a one-hop SIMILAR_TO expansion of seeds (weight >= threshold, capped) before render recovers fragment-split evidence pre-merge
- **Prediction** - would have flipped P09/P16 WITHOUT the H365 hoist (testable retroactively: rollback marker exists); on the current graph, flat-to-positive with the hoist already in place
- **Acceptance bar** - offline A/B including the retro-test (H365 marks removed, expansion on): P09/P16 flip via the SIMILAR_TO bridge alone -> CONFIRMED as the general mechanism (H365's hoist becomes the special case); zero regressions; REFUTED if expansion floods the render budget (fully_covered drops)
- **Experiment** - offline, config-flagged; requires a temporary H365 rollback for the retro-arm (marks are one-shot reversible); source: HippoRAG digest + densify.py capability map
- **Result** - forward arm ([`r34-h370-simexpand-20260710T162121Z.json`](../../reports/r34-h370-simexpand-20260710T162121Z.json), `scripts/r34_h370_similar_expand.py`; thresholds 0.15/0.20/0.25 inside the pile's observed weight range 0.138-0.276, cap 8): DEAD FLAT - 0.8542/20 at every threshold, expansions tiny (0.1-1.3 added seeds per probe, +0.4% to +7.7% context). Retro-arm pre-check kills the decisive test without touching the graph: ZERO SIMILAR_TO edges exist on the fragment trio ('HC230-Series', 'HC230 Product Range', 'Sleep Style 200 Series') - the three creators linked HC23x child models to EACH OTHER (10+ edges among HC231-238) but never bridged the series-level fragments, so expansion structurally cannot recover P09/P16 pre-hoist; the H365 rollback would measure nothing
- **Verdict** - **REFUTED (2026-07-10)** - one-hop SIMILAR_TO expansion earns no render access on this graph: flat forward, and the retro-arm's bridge edge does not exist. The sharper finding is about the CREATORS, not the consumer: the defer soft-links and kNN densify covered the easy same-tier duplicates and missed the parent-tier fragment pair that actually cost recall - the H364/H365 class is invisible to embedding-kNN at the thresholds used. SIMILAR_TO's creation cost remains unjustified by any read path (the capability-map question stands; candidate input for R36-H380's worth pricing)

**Composed frontier (2026-07-10, round close)** - the confirmed levers measured stacked ([`r34-composed-frontier-20260710T162930Z.json`](../../reports/r34-composed-frontier-20260710T162930Z.json), `scripts/r34_composed_frontier.py`): engine-parity retrieval (overfetch 64 -> 16, DEF-14) 0.8958 / 21 of 24; + H367-B proposition seeds 0.9167 / 22 (+18.3% ctx); + H366 s900k1 window 0.9583 / **23 of 24** (+27.8% ctx - inside the 30% budget even stacked). Zero regressions at every rung. The single non-full probe is P08 (reader parse loss - unreachable by any retrieval-side lever, awaiting the parser class). Escalation-need decomposition: only P15 and P22 (8.3% of probes) required the levers beyond parity - the observation that seeds R37-H382. Engine wiring tranche: DEF-14 harness parity mode, H367 routing (one line, pipeline.py:1085), H366 span render + passage index on the acc-crit Embeddings provider abstraction.

**Engine wiring tranche LANDED (2026-07-10 evening)** - all three items shipped: (1) the acc-crit Embeddings provider abstraction (`embed_channel_texts` with bedrock / local-gpu / openai providers, `ChannelEmbedding` + `EmbeddingChannels` settings, device pinning with fail-fast edges, `channel_dimensions` derived from the model) - 8 of 10 acc-crit criteria implemented, 2 honestly partial (space provenance on legacy channels, NV-Embed-v2 verification rides #59); (2) H366 in the engine: `graph/passages.py` (span construction reproduces the offline cache keying `{chunk_id}:900:{start}` exactly, `(:KGFPassage)` store idempotent, `(:KGFIndexSpace)` registry with query-time `check_space` refusal), `optimize()` builds passages under `passages_enabled`, `_retrieve_local` appends the top query-anchored span under escalation (rung 2 live); (3) DEF-14 fixed: engine parity (overfetch) is now the harness DEFAULT with `overfetch_factor` stamped in every report. 418 tests + 9 live integration green. Parity caveat for the H385 replay-match clause: engine rung 1 routes prop-seeded entities ADDITIVELY (scored by prop hit, ranked by render_budget) where the offline arm used a displacement union (parity head-12 + up to 4) and ranked propositions in bge-m3 space vs the engine's Titan proposition index - the replay match will adjudicate whether the constructions are recall-equivalent

## R35 - associative query memory: the graph pre-computes its own questions at ingest (registered 2026-07-10, user directive via /datascience:hypothesis, research by 2 primed agents)

The user's concept: at ingestion time, create queries (and possibly other objects) associated with ingested content - queue them, rank them, index them - so query time becomes query-to-query matching. The literature pass found a mature lineage with verified numbers: doc2query (+3.1 MRR@10, +4.0 R@1000 MS MARCO; 31% of generated query terms are vocabulary the source never contains), Doc2Query-- (quality-gating generated queries: +16% effectiveness AND -33% index simultaneously - unfiltered generation is strictly worse), QuOTE question embeddings (+10.5 SQuAD / +12.5 MultiHop-RAG top-k context accuracy at 141ms/query - 9x cheaper than HyDE), Dense X propositions (+9 to +12 recall@5; caveat: paraphrase objects collapse citation accuracy per the Extractive-Abstractive Spectrum - generated objects must be ALIASES with provenance, never replacement evidence), Anthropic contextual retrieval (-49% retrieval failures, vendor-measured), sleep-time compute (offline query anticipation, ~5x test-time compute reduction). Budget consensus across papers: 5-15 fixed-count questions per chunk, groundedness-filtered, diminishing returns beyond. The Hawkins pass (2 Numenta papers archived) grounds the frame honestly: HTM's engineering record is thin (flagged third-party NAB re-runs put deep learning ahead), so nothing is adopted on theory - but prediction-as-recognition (memory = matching input against pre-generated expectations) is exactly this round made falsifiable, and thousand-brains voting survives as multi-view retrieval consensus. Naive baseline: same as R34 (post-H365 live graph, 0.8542, failed mass 3.5 = P08/P15/P21/P22). Papers: doc2query, Doc2Query--, Dense X Retrieval, RAPTOR, QuOTE, Thousands of Synapses Sequence Memory, Grid Cells Cortical Reference Frames - all archived with digests in `references/papers/`.

### R35-H371 Question nodes - ingest-time expectation objects as first-class retrieval targets

- **Persona** - conformist (the round's centerpiece; three independent published lineages - doc2query, QuOTE, QuIM - plus the Hawkins prediction-as-recognition frame all converge on it)
- **Grounding** - QuOTE +10.5/+12.5 context accuracy; doc2query's 31% novel-vocabulary injection attacks the never-emitted-phrases class by construction; Doc2Query-- proves the groundedness gate is mandatory (+16%/-33%); HippoRAG-2's +12.5 from query-to-triple shows compact generated objects beat raw text as seed anchors; KGF schema: `(:Question {text, embedding})-[:ANSWERABLE_FROM]->(:Chunk)` + `[:ABOUT]->(:Entity)`
- **Hypothesis** - generating a fixed 8 questions per chunk at ingest, gating each with a groundedness check (does the chunk answer it?), and seeding retrieval on the question index recovers vocabulary-gap failures that neither extraction nor passage embedding reaches
- **Prediction** - on the live graph: P21/P22 flip (their phrases answer obvious generated questions); mean recall >= +0.05 over the post-H366 state (composes with passage nodes); index grows <= 8x entries but questions are ~15 tokens each
- **Acceptance bar** - offline-ish prototype: generate questions for the 247 chunks on the local engine post-H363 (idle-card economics), embed via Titan, extend harness seeding; both probes flip with zero regressions AND the gain survives WITH H366 active (else H366 subsumes it - record honestly); Doc2Query-- clause: the gated set must match or beat the ungated set at smaller index
- **Experiment** - generation on the local model after the ramp frees the card; embedding + measure offline; sources: QuOTE, doc2query, Doc2Query-- digests + PDFs

### R35-H372 Entity-scoped cross-document questions - the only object that spans documents by construction

- **Persona** - heretical (H354 proved NO chunking, splitting, or windowing can co-locate cross-document evidence; this is the one registered mechanism that can - because the question is generated over the entity's merged multi-document evidence, not over any single text)
- **Grounding** - P15 ('1.98kg' DreamStation PDF vs '2.4 kg' BMC docs) carries 10% of residual mass, unreachable by any splitter (H354 verdict); KGF entities already aggregate source_documents provenance across docs; a second generation pass over entity-merged evidence yields questions like 'how do the weights of X and Y compare' whose embeddings sit exactly where comparison queries land
- **Hypothesis** - a per-entity (or per-entity-pair, co-typed) question pass over merged cross-document evidence, indexed as Question nodes with ABOUT edges to both entities, flips the cross-document comparison class
- **Prediction** - P15 0.5 -> 1.0; the mechanism generalizes to the benchmark corpora's bridge questions (2Wiki structure)
- **Acceptance bar** - GATED on H371 passing its gate (shared machinery); P15 flips with zero regressions; budget clause: entity-pair fanout capped by co-type + shared-doc-neighborhood blocking (no quadratic generation)
- **Experiment** - extends H371's generation pass with an entity-scoped second stage; sources: QuOTE digest + H354 census verdict

### R35-H373 Questions as audit probes - the gap ledger gets its generator

- **Persona** - follower (operationalizes the user's 'queue them, rank them' directly, and the self-auditing-foundry doctrine gets its missing input side)
- **Grounding** - sleep-time compute (offline anticipation of probable queries, verified ~5x test-compute reduction on stateful benches); TCR-QF/EvoRAG use failed queries as repair triggers (archived digests); KGF doctrine: the graph is a quality controller - a generated question that FAILS its own answerability gate against the graph is precisely a gap-ledger entry (the P08 '9.0W' parse loss would surface as an unanswerable power-consumption question, today invisible until a user asks)
- **Hypothesis** - the H371 generation pass double-writes: answerable questions become retrieval targets; unanswerable-but-grounded questions (chunk supports it, graph cannot answer it) enter a ranked gap ledger that names extraction/parse losses without any probe set
- **Prediction** - the ledger's top entries rediscover the known failure classes blind (P08 parse loss, pre-H365 spec unreachability) - the census results become machine-generated
- **Acceptance bar** - ledger precision proxy: >= 50% of top-20 ledger entries correspond to verifiable graph gaps (manual adjudication once); REFUTED if the ledger is dominated by generation noise (ungrounded questions passing the gate)
- **Experiment** - same run as H371, one extra gate per question (answerability check against the graph = one retrieval + present-check, no LLM); sources: sleep-time compute + TCR-QF digests

### R35-H374 Multi-view retrieval voting - thousand-brains consensus over independent channels

- **Persona** - contrarian (the channels exist - entity seeds, proposition seeds, question seeds, SIMILAR_TO expansion - and each is currently a silo or dormant; Hawkins' voting says consensus across independent partial views beats any single view)
- **Grounding** - thousand-brains voting (Grid Cells paper, archived - flagged theory-not-benchmark); ensemble consensus is independently sound; H364's failure shape is exactly a single-channel failure (vector seeds found the right subjects, nothing corroborated the carrier entities); HippoRAG-1's uncertainty ensemble (+2.3/+2.9 on two benches) is the nearest published relative
- **Hypothesis** - ranking candidate entities by cross-channel agreement (how many independent views surface them) improves precision at flat recall - wrong-entity spec hits fail corroboration
- **Prediction** - fully_covered rises or holds with fewer render-budget evictions; precision proxy improves on the fidelity probes
- **Acceptance bar** - offline A/B once >= 3 channels are live (post H366/H367/H371): zero recall regressions, measurable precision or evacuation gain; REFUTED if consensus just recapitulates the vector ranking (rank correlation > 0.95 - then voting is theater, H37-style)
- **Experiment** - pure re-ranking arithmetic over existing channel outputs, offline; sources: Grid Cells digest + HippoRAG digest App F.2
- **Result (context side, 2026-07-10 night)** - ([`r35-h374-consensus-20260710T204545Z.json`](../../reports/r35-h374-consensus-20260710T204545Z.json), `scripts/r35_h374_consensus.py`) three live channels voted per probe (entity/Titan kNN as the fixed control; propositions and 900-char spans both in bge-m3 space from the r34 caches, GPU 2 for the 24 query embeds), consensus ordered by (agreement votes, reciprocal-rank fusion). The theater clause did NOT fire: mean Spearman vs the entity ranking is 0.5494 (threshold 0.95) with render turnover 8-10 of 16 per probe - the channels genuinely disagree and consensus genuinely reorders. But recall is BIT-IDENTICAL: 0.8958 both arms, 21/24 fully covered both arms, zero regressions and zero gains. Reading: half the render is fungible - swapping 8-10 tail entities for cross-channel-corroborated ones changes no probe's coverage, an H37-shaped finding (the vector head carries the recall; the tail is interchangeable). Note on the baseline: 0.8958 is the DEF-14 parity-depth instrument (deeper ANN fetch improves the head); the r34 offline 0.8542 was the pre-parity factor=1 form
- **Verdict deferred to the precision arm** - the zero-regression clause and the not-theater clause both hold, but the registered "measurable precision or evacuation gain" cannot be assessed on gold-recall alone when the swapped tail carries no golds either way; the precision proxy (fewer wrong-entity spec hits in render) needs the fidelity probes / LLM judge, post-H363

### R35-H375 SDR-overlap identity signatures - sparse content-addressable recall under extraction variance

- **Persona** - heretical (a different similarity substrate for the identity problem: bit-overlap on salient-token signatures vs cosine on dense embeddings; Hawkins' one analytically-proven artifact applied to KGF's measured worst surface)
- **Grounding** - SDR union/overlap math is analytically proven (Thousands of Synapses digest, archived); H107: 71% of the identity problem is extraction variance - two extractions of one entity drift in dense space but share salient attribute/relation tokens; the H364 fragment pair shared 'HC230' + PART_OF children - a signature overlap no cosine threshold caught
- **Hypothesis** - sparse binary signatures (bit-set over salient name/attribute/relation tokens) recall same-entity pairs under extraction variance better than cosine at matched false-match rate
- **Prediction** - on the H107 66-pair same-type benchmark: signature overlap recalls >= 10% more true pairs at equal false-match budget than the shipped cosine blocking
- **Acceptance bar** - offline head-to-head on the existing pair benchmark (no ingest, no GPU); wins -> registers as a candidate-generation stage for the resolver (NOT a replacement for the Bayesian posterior); loses -> closed, Hawkins substrate retired
- **Experiment** - pure offline computation over existing graph entities; sources: Thousands of Synapses digest + H107 verdict
- **Result (2026-07-10 night)** - ([`r35-h375-sdr-20260710T204300Z.json`](../../reports/r35-h375-sdr-20260710T204300Z.json), `scripts/r35_h375_sdr.py`) head-to-head candidate generation over all 5,213 entities: cosine kNN (the shipped blocking's machinery, 37,009 candidates) vs bit-overlap on salient-token signatures (name tokens + model codes + prop keys/values + PART_OF-neighbour codes, df-capped at 50; ~59.8k candidates). Benchmark decay first: only 14 of 68 true pairs are still name-resolvable on the current pile (12 forensics + 2 fragment pairs; the other 54 were merged or renamed by the intervening layered ingests). The first pass looked like an SDR win (fragment pairs at rank 40/41, 4-vs-1 at N=500) - but that was CONTAMINATION: the pile carries the H365 repair marks, and the bridge edges + hoisted props create exactly the shared-neighbour tokens the signature needs. The honest PRE-REPAIR arm (h365 edges excluded, hoisted props stripped) shows the HC230 fragment pair at rank 58,062 of 59,761 and the SleepStyle pair not a candidate at all - pre-repair, the fragments shared only the bare 'hc230' token because the childless fragment had NO children and NO props; the class is structurally token-poor, which is WHY nothing caught it. Benchmark recall pre-repair is mixed and fails the +10% bar: N=500 2-vs-1, N=1000 2-vs-3, N=2000 4-vs-3, N=5000 4-vs-5, N=10000 5-vs-8 (cosine wins at scale)
- **Verdict** - **REFUTED** - per the registered disposition, the Hawkins substrate is retired. Candidate generation must find pairs BEFORE any repair, and pre-repair the SDR signature carries no more signal than cosine on the surviving benchmark while decisively failing the fragment class that motivated it. Corollary worth keeping: POST-repair the signature ranks the fragments top-41 - token signatures can VERIFY structural repairs cheaply but cannot DISCOVER them; discovery stays with the H365 stem+marker rule and the H119 canonicalization track

| Hypothesis | Persona | Lever | Grounding size | Pre-reg prediction | Acceptance bar |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R35-H371 | conformist | question nodes, gated, fixed-8 | +10.5/+12.5 (QuOTE), +16/-33 (D2Q--) | P21/P22 flip, >= +0.05 over H366 | flip + zero regressions + survives-with-H366 + gate clause |
| R35-H372 | heretical | entity-scoped cross-doc questions | H354: class unreachable by splitters | P15 -> 1.0 | gated on H371; flip + capped fanout |
| R35-H373 | follower | questions double as gap-ledger probes | sleep-time ~5x; TCR-QF pattern | ledger rediscovers known gaps blind | >= 50% of top-20 entries verify |
| R35-H374 | contrarian | multi-view consensus ranking | ensemble-sound; H1 ensemble +2.3/+2.9 | precision up at flat recall | >= 3 channels; rank-corr < 0.95 else theater |
| R35-H375 | heretical | SDR bit-overlap identity signatures | SDR math proven; H107 71% variance | +10% pair recall at equal FMR | wins -> candidate stage; loses -> retire Hawkins substrate |

Defects: DEF-13 (H371/H372 target the emission residue and cross-document classes; H373 generates the gap ledger that makes DEF-13-style residue self-surfacing), DEF-11 tangentially (question objects are naming-churn-immune retrieval anchors). Sequencing: H371 generation needs the local card (post-H363); everything else in the round is offline or rides H371's artifacts. Cross-round rule: H366 (passages) runs FIRST so H371's bar can honestly separate question-node gains from passage-node gains.

## R36 - maintenance of derived objects: nothing invalidates anything, and the literature says invalidate eagerly, regenerate lazily (registered 2026-07-10, user directive, research by 2 primed agents)

The user's order: fanout maintenance hypotheses for the generated/derived objects - extensive research first. Two agents delivered: an external literature pass (incremental view maintenance, truth-maintenance systems, incremental GraphRAG, embedding staleness, agentic memory, generated-query staleness) and an internal cartographer pass over the shipped engine; both briefs persisted verbatim ([`r36-maintenance-literature-brief-20260710.md`](../../reports/r36-maintenance-literature-brief-20260710.md), [`r36-maintenance-internal-map-20260710.md`](../../reports/r36-maintenance-internal-map-20260710.md); new papers archived with digests: EraRAG, Sleep-time Compute, Mem0, A-MEM, DBSP). The internal finding is stark: derived-object invalidation DOES NOT EXIST in the engine for any class - stale propositions stay indexed after merges, community summaries of dissolved communities keep serving, SIMILAR_TO edges are never re-scored after embedding overwrites, re-ingested edited documents orphan their old chunks forever; the only refresh mechanism is "run optimize() again and hope". Two adjacent corpses: the RECURING FSM state is a dead end (`end_recure` has zero production callers) and the bitemporal fact-drift alarm is dead by default (`functional_relationship_types=[]` means `valid_to` is NEVER set, so the R8 alarm structurally cannot fire). The literature's consistent pattern: justification records on every derived object (TMS), dirty-label floods through the dependency DAG, invalidate eagerly / regenerate lazily (LLM regeneration is 10-100x revalidation cost), verifier gate before re-admission - and five slots verified OPEN (generated-question revalidation, justification-carrying retrieval objects, merge/split fallout, outcome-linked worth on index objects, gap-ledger lifecycle). Dedup fence: R28 owns the drift-trigger repair (H317 CUSUM - CONFIRMED, unwired = DEF-9), monitors, Gap/Demand worklists, provenance stamps, and repair economics; R15-R17 own idempotency, compaction, retraction, and entity versioning. R36 claims ONLY: derived-object invalidation as a class, the RECURING dead-end, the dead-by-default alarm, and maintenance of the NEW R34/R35 object classes (passage embeddings, question nodes) which nothing else claims. Naive baseline: the shipped engine - staleness detection rate 0% for every derived-object class, repair only by full re-run.

### R36-H376 Justification-carrying derived objects - the dependency DAG makes staleness detectable

- **Persona** - conformist (the literature's core recipe, 45 years old and never applied to a KG-RAG index: JTMS justifications + label propagation, Build-Systems verifying traces, Zep's bitemporal invalidation for facts only)
- **Grounding** - JTMS/ATMS: belief carries justification set, support falls -> label flips OUT, retraction exact and automatic; Zep DMR 94.8% on the invalidate-don't-delete pole; LightRAG issue #2442 as the negative lesson (derivation caches keyed without upstream state propagate rot); internal map §4: the invalidation matrix is empty for ALL object classes
- **Hypothesis** - recording justification = {source doc version, entity ids, description hash, generator prompt+model, embedding version} on every derived object and flooding dirty labels through the dependency DAG on change events (entity merge, description rewrite, doc re-version) detects the stale derived objects that today go entirely undetected
- **Prediction** - on a replayed change set (the H365 repair events + one synthetic doc re-version), the flood flags >= 95% of truly-affected derived objects (hand-adjudicated on the HC230 neighborhood), over-invalidation bounded DRed-style (false-dirty <= 2x true-dirty, all revalidatable)
- **Acceptance bar** - offline prototype on the live pile (justifications reconstructed from provenance already stored; marker property, no deletions): >= 95% true-dirty coverage, false-dirty <= 2x; OUT-labeling the flagged set from render costs ZERO recall on unaffected probes; REFUTED if existing provenance is too thin to reconstruct justifications (then the finding is an acc-crit item: justifications must be written at creation)
- **Experiment** - offline, graph reads + marker writes; sources: both r36 briefs (TMS cards f1/f2, build-systems card a7)
- **Result (2026-07-10 night)** - ([`r36-h376-justifications-20260710T203351Z.json`](../../reports/r36-h376-justifications-20260710T203351Z.json), `scripts/r36_h376_justifications.py`) reconstruction census: provenance on the pile is THICK - entity descriptions 5213/5213 reconstructable ({source_documents, source_chunks} present on every entity), SIMILAR_TO 573/573 (endpoint ids), KGFPassage 4253/4253 (chunk-id keying + emb_provider/emb_model stamps) - the registered REFUTED-thin clause cannot fire; the ONE thin field is hoisted `prop_*` (2 objects, 0 reconstructable: the `spec_hoisted` marker names keys but not source entities). Field-level flood on the two registered events: E1 (H365 prop change) flags ZERO SIMILAR_TO edges (props do not feed embeddings - the DRed-style discipline holds at field granularity); E2 (synthetic re-version of the SleepStyle_200 manual) floods 8 chunks -> 132 passages + 148 entities (92 single-doc) + 12 SIMILAR_TO (9 strict) = ~5.4% of derived objects, a bounded blast radius. Adjudication: object-level coverage 1.0 (bar >= 0.95), false-dirty/true-dirty 0.2511 (bar <= 2). Honesty caveat: the two hoisted-prop objects are caught only because their parents happen to be D-sourced (object-level ride-along); at field level they are structurally invisible (census 0/2) - had the parents not been D-sourced, coverage lands 233/235 = 99.1% (still over bar). Render clause measured live: OUT-labeling all 148 flagged entities regresses ONLY P09 (1.0 -> 0.0) and P16 (1.0 -> 0.5) - both in the affected set (their golds ARE the hoisted specs from D); unaffected-probe regressions EMPTY (mean 0.8542 -> 0.8333, the delta fully attributable)
- **Verdict** - **CONFIRMED** (coverage 1.0 >= 95%, false-dirty 0.25x <= 2x, zero unaffected-probe recall cost, field-level discipline exact on E1). The substrate stands: justifications ARE reconstructable from shipped provenance for every stored class except hoist-derived fields - which is measured evidence for the registered cross-round acc-crit rule (derived objects MUST write justification records at creation; retrofit cannot recover the hoist DAG). H377 consumes this dirty-set post-H363; H380 prices the same objects

### R36-H377 Revalidation, not regeneration - the groundedness gate re-run as a staleness detector

- **Persona** - heretical (verified-open slot: nobody has published revalidating stored generated objects against edited sources; the gate exists only as a generation-time filter)
- **Grounding** - Doc2Query-- gate: +16% effectiveness AND -33% index at generation time - the identical check ("does the current source still support this object?") is an entailment call; regeneration is ~16x base indexing cost (QuOTE), revalidation ~10-100x cheaper; Salsa early-cutoff needs a semantic predicate for LLM output - unpublished
- **Hypothesis** - for dirty-flagged derived text objects (propositions now, question nodes post-H371), an entailment re-check against the current source decides keep-vs-regenerate, matching regenerate-from-scratch decisions at a fraction of the cost - most dirty objects survive their trigger (semantic early cutoff)
- **Prediction** - >= 90% decision agreement with a regenerate-and-compare reference on a sampled dirty set; >= 50% of dirty objects rescued (kept); measured cost <= 1/10 of regeneration
- **Acceptance bar** - sampled A/B on H376's dirty set: both the agreement and cost clauses hold; REFUTED if agreement < 80% (gate unreliable -> policy falls back to regenerate-always and only the lazy scheduling survives)
- **Experiment** - offline; entailment checks on the local engine post-H363 (idle-card economics); sources: literature brief area (e), Doc2Query-- digest

### R36-H378 Wire the confirmed drift trigger and give RECURING an exit - DEF-9's workstream

- **Persona** - follower (composition of a CONFIRMED lever with a dead FSM path; no new mechanism, only the closing of two known loops)
- **Grounding** - H317 CUSUM drift trigger CONFIRMED in R28 and never wired (= DEF-9); internal map: `end_recure` has zero production callers - the FSM can enter RECURING and never leave; GraphRAG's three-tier escalation (patch -> partial recompute -> full rebuild) as the shape of the consolidation pass
- **Hypothesis** - wiring the CUSUM trigger to flip the FSM into RECURING plus implementing a bounded consolidation pass that exits it repairs induced drift without a full rebuild
- **Prediction** - on the R28 drift replay: alarm fires, FSM walks STABLE -> RECURING -> STABLE, the drifted region is repaired, and the 24-probe harness shows zero regression
- **Acceptance bar** - end-to-end induced-drift replay passes all three clauses and DEF-9 closes; REFUTED-as-designed if consolidation cost approaches full-rebuild cost (the tier is then theater and the honest fix is scheduled rebuilds)
- **Experiment** - engine change behind a config flag + replay on scratch; sources: internal map §2-§3, R28/H317 verdict
- **Result (wiring + unit verdicts, 2026-07-10 night)** - both corpses closed in the engine behind `drift.cusum_enabled` (default off until the replay): (1) the H317 CUSUM ported verbatim into `DriftDetector._cusum_evaluate` - one-sided S = max(0, S + (x - (mu0 + k))) over the dense per-document JSD series, mu0 armed from the first window's median, k = 0.02 / h = 0.30 (the H317 constants), reset-on-fire, state persisted through `to_dict`/`from_dict` (the detector remains a deterministic left-fold, H317's session-survival property); (2) the RECURING exit: `end_recure` gets its first production caller - after `window` post-recure documents, `adopt_drifted_types` adopts into the cured ontology the register's sustained types (>= `recure_adopt_share` of window mass, patch tier of the GraphRAG escalation), the detector re-baselines on the observed window and re-arms, `lifecycle.cure()` walks RECURING -> STABLE. Unit verdicts pin the DEF-9 anatomy: the anti-phase case (drifted register at sub-threshold remap rates) fires CUSUM and provably CANNOT fire the boolean (tested both ways); slow-ramp fires; stationary never fires; the livelock guard holds - after the exit, the SAME register no longer alarms (adoption + re-baseline). 437 tests green. Remaining per the bar: the end-to-end induced-drift replay on scratch (alarm -> RECURING -> STABLE with the 24-probe harness flat) needs drift-inducing ingest - post-H363; DEF-9 stays open until that replay passes

### R36-H379 Resurrect the dead fact-drift alarm - infer functional types instead of asking the operator

- **Persona** - contrarian (the alarm's machinery is built and sound; the default config makes it structurally dead - the fix is deriving the config from the graph, not documenting the knob)
- **Grounding** - internal map: `functional_relationship_types=[]` -> `valid_to` never set anywhere -> R8 alarm cannot fire; Zep/Graphiti invalidate contradicted edges bitemporally at ingest; ATOM's temporal 5-tuples show single-valued (functional) relations are inferable from data
- **Hypothesis** - inferring functional relationship types from observed cardinality profiles (a relation that is single-valued per subject across the graph is functional) activates bitemporal invalidation with zero manual configuration
- **Prediction** - on a two-version doc replay (a spec value changes between versions), the superseded edge gets `valid_to` set and the alarm fires; zero false invalidations on legitimately multi-valued types (gold edges intact)
- **Acceptance bar** - replay passes both clauses; REFUTED if cardinality inference mislabels multi-valued types on the live pile (any gold edge falsely invalidated) - then functional typing stays manual and the verdict names the reason
- **Experiment** - offline inference over the live graph + replay on scratch; sources: internal map §3, Zep + ATOM digests
- **Result (inference arm, 2026-07-10)** - ([`r36-h379-cardinality-20260710T173206Z.json`](../../reports/r36-h379-cardinality-20260710T173206Z.json), `scripts/r36_h379_cardinality.py`) 28 live relationship types profiled on the pile; `valid_to` is absent from EVERY relationship (the server warns the property key does not exist graph-wide) - direct confirmation the alarm is structurally dead. Graph-wide single-valued fractions are far weaker than the hypothesis assumed: only MANUFACTURED_BY clears 90% (90.22%, yet max fanout 5 - a product with five distinct live manufacturers is H107-class identity noise, not a fact), and NO type clears 95%. The semantically functional tier sits below every bar (BELONGS_TO_CATEGORY 86.9%, IS_MODEL_OF 85.9%, PART_OF 84.9%) - fanout inflated by extraction/identity variance. Reading: naive graph-wide cardinality inference UNDER-labels true functional types on a noisy pile (the inverse failure of the registered REFUTED clause). The replay arm proceeds with the @0.90 candidate set {MANUFACTURED_BY}; if the machinery confirms, the follow-up estimator is noise-robust (per-subject mode share, or profiles recomputed post-H365 repair)

### R36-H380 Outcome-linked worth - the judge's verdicts price every derived object for free

- **Persona** - conformist (published and strong for agent memories - Memory-Worth rho 0.89 vs 0.00 for static importance - never applied to retrieval index objects; the counters are near-free)
- **Grounding** - When-to-Forget: two counters per object (co-occurrence with pass vs fail outcomes) converge to conditional success probability, rho 0.89 +- 0.02 after 10k episodes, stale objects fall to 0.17 while load-bearing hold 0.77; KGF's harness already produces per-probe verdicts every run - the outcome stream exists and is discarded
- **Hypothesis** - attaching pass/fail co-retrieval counters to derived objects (entities in render, propositions, SIMILAR_TO edges) over replayed harness runs yields a worth signal that separates stale/harmful objects from load-bearing ones better than any age/TTL rule
- **Prediction** - after >= 20 replayed probe runs: evacuating the bottom-decile-worth objects from render costs ZERO recall; the known-superseded objects (pre-H365 fragment carriers) land in the bottom quartile
- **Acceptance bar** - both clauses on the replay ledger; REFUTED if worth ranking is uninformative (rank correlation with adjudicated staleness < 0.3) - then outcome linkage needs denser traffic than a 24-probe harness provides and the verdict says so
- **Experiment** - pure offline arithmetic over harness run logs (replays are cheap, no LLM); sources: literature brief card d5
- **Result (2026-07-10 night)** - ([`r36-h380-worth-20260710T203613Z.json`](../../reports/r36-h380-worth-20260710T203613Z.json), `scripts/r36_h380_worth.py`) 96 episodes (24 probes x 4 context budgets k in {8,12,16,24} - retrieval is deterministic per config, so budget variation supplies the episode diversity), 278 entities priced by Laplace-smoothed pass/fail co-retrieval counters. Worth spread is real: 0.167 bottom to 0.944 top. Clause (a) PASSED exactly: evacuating the bottom decile (27 entities) from the k=16 render costs zero recall (mean 0.8958 -> 0.8958, no probe regresses). Clause (b) UNMEASURABLE: 'HC230 Product Range' - the adjudicated-superseded carrier - is retrieved in ZERO of 96 episodes; an object with no traffic has no counters, so no worth rank can place it in any quartile. Found nuance: at harness density the bottom decile is not stale objects but FAILURE-CLUSTER NEIGHBORS - 'PowerConsumption' (worth 0.167) sits in P08's parse-loss neighborhood, co-retrieved only by the probe that always fails; the counters correctly localize failure-adjacent context, which is gap-ledger signal (H373's domain), not staleness signal
- **Verdict** - **REFUTED on the registered bar at harness traffic density** (both clauses were required; the staleness-separation clause cannot fire when superseded objects receive no retrieval traffic - the registration's own anticipated failure: 'outcome linkage needs denser traffic than a 24-probe harness provides'). Clause (a) stands independently: bottom-decile evacuation is recall-free, and the counters are near-free telemetry worth carrying. Disposition: no pruning policy ships on worth at this density; revisit with production query volumes or the #59 benchmark's query stream, where superseded objects can actually accumulate counters

### R36-H381 Gap-ledger lifecycle - answerability re-probing closes gaps and grades the ingest

- **Persona** - follower (inverts H373's generator into a maintainer; verified-open slot - a maintained gap ledger appears nowhere in the literature)
- **Grounding** - TCR-QF: failed queries drive knowledge incorporation, 29.1% EM / 15.5% F1 over GraphRAG baselines (IJCAI 2025) - run in reverse: open gaps re-probed as ingest lands; sleep-time compute 2.5x amortization prices the idle loop; Zep invalidate-don't-delete: a closed gap is a record ("unanswerable from doc-set X until event Y"), which is abstention-calibration signal
- **Hypothesis** - a periodic re-probe of open gap entries against the current graph closes gaps the moment new ingest answers them, with closure recorded as invalidation (never deletion) that doubles as an ingest-quality event stream
- **Prediction** - injecting one document that answers 2 known open gaps closes exactly those 2 on the next re-probe cycle; untouched gaps stay open; each closure record carries the answering event's provenance
- **Acceptance bar** - GATED on H373 passing (the ledger must exist); exact closure of the injected pair with zero spurious closures; the re-probe cycle costs one retrieval + one present-check per gap (no LLM)
- **Experiment** - rides H373's artifacts + one injected document; loop runs offline; sources: literature brief cards on TCR-QF/sleep-time, gap-ledger doctrine

| R36-H377 | heretical | entailment gate as staleness revalidator | D2Q-- +16/-33; regen ~16x cost | >= 90% agreement, >= 50% rescued, <= 1/10 cost | all three clauses; < 80% agreement -> regenerate-always |
| R36-H378 | follower | wire H317 CUSUM + RECURING exit (DEF-9) | H317 CONFIRMED; end_recure 0 callers | drift replay: alarm -> RECURING -> STABLE, repaired | replay end-to-end + zero probe regression |

Defects: DEF-9 (H378 IS the workstream that closes it), DEF-13 tangentially (H376/H377 keep the repaired spec surfaces from silently rotting when sources change).
