## R37 - calibrated progressive context: the LLM asks for more only where it pays (registered 2026-07-10, user directive)

The user's concept, verbatim intent: "what if the LLM was to decide if it needs more context (progressively), and we calibrated it such that it happens only where worth the effort - and not too often?" The R34 composed frontier makes this precisely quantifiable: the static composed render reaches 0.9583 / 23-of-24 but pays +27.8% context on EVERY probe, while the escalation-need decomposition shows only P15 and P22 (8.3% of probes) actually required the levers beyond parity retrieval. A calibrated escalation trigger that fires on exactly the needy probes would buy the frontier recall at a few percent average growth. KGF already owns most of the substrate: the R19-H181 miss detector is a shipped, free, seed-similarity abstention gate (the rung-0 sufficiency signal candidate); R27-H281 measured 85.6% of escalation decisions as cheap-decidable (the economics of NOT invoking the expensive path); H157's per-corpus self-calibration provides the threshold-fitting machinery. External lineage (Self-RAG reflection tokens, FLARE active retrieval, Adaptive-RAG complexity routing) is cited by name only - the papers are archived per the paper rule before the experiment runs, and no number from them is load-bearing here. Naive baselines (both poles): always-escalate = static compose (0.9583 at +27.8%); never-escalate = parity rung 0 (0.8958 at +0%). H382 must beat BOTH on the recall-per-context frontier.

### R37-H382 Calibrated escalation ladder - sufficiency-gated progressive context

- **Persona** - follower (the user's mechanism, made falsifiable; every rung already exists as a measured lever, only the trigger is new)
- **Grounding** - composed frontier: rung deltas are measured (parity 0.8958 -> +props 0.9167 -> +window 0.9583) and the true-need set is known (P15, P22 = 8.3%); H181 miss detector ships a free sufficiency proxy (top seed similarity); H281: 85.6% of escalation decisions cheap-decidable; the LLM-self-report variant runs on the local engine (idle-window economics, post-H363)
- **Hypothesis** - a per-query sufficiency signal evaluated on the rung-0 render, calibrated on held-out probes, escalates to rung 1 (proposition-seed union) and rung 2 (query-anchored window) ONLY where the context is actually insufficient - recovering the composed-frontier recall at a fraction of its average context cost
- **Prediction** - recall matches the static compose (0.9583, 23/24, zero regressions vs rung 0); mean context growth <= 10% (vs +27.8% static); escalation rate <= 30% of queries with the true-need probes (P15/P22 class) ALWAYS escalated; signal comparison: the LLM self-report beats the free H181 threshold on escalation precision or it is not worth its latency
- **Acceptance bar** - offline ladder on the pile with three signal arms (H181 top-score threshold | LLM self-report "is the answer present in this context?" on the local model | always-escalate control): frontier recall AND <= half of static-compose average growth AND escalation rate <= 30%; REFUTED if no calibratable signal separates need from no-need (uniform or inverted escalation - then static compose is the honest ship and the trigger idea dies); calibration clause: threshold fitted on a probe split, reported on the holdout (no self-fitting)
- **Experiment** - offline; rungs replay from the existing caches (span + proposition embeddings), sufficiency LLM calls on gpt-oss-120b post-H363; lineage papers (Self-RAG, FLARE, Adaptive-RAG) archived with digests before the run; sources: composed-frontier report, H181/H281/H157 verdicts
- **Result (arm 1: H181 threshold + control poles, 2026-07-10)** - ([`r37-h382-ladder-arm1-20260710T173529Z.json`](../../reports/r37-h382-ladder-arm1-20260710T173529Z.json), `scripts/r37_h382_ladder.py`; lineage papers archived: Self-RAG / FLARE / Adaptive-RAG digests in references/papers/) 2-fold cross-calibration with P15 and P22 split across folds; fitted thresholds nearly identical (0.7650 / 0.7644 - the signal cut is stable). POOLED HOLDOUT: mean recall **0.9583, 23/24 fully covered** - exactly the always-escalate frontier - at context growth **+6.9%** (vs +27.8% static, a 4x cost reduction) and escalation rate **25%** (6 of 24: P01, P06, P08, P12, P15, P22). Both true-need probes escalated out-of-fold; the 4 surplus escalations cost only context, zero recall harm (P08 among them - escalation is wasted there but harmless). Every registered clause met by the FREE signal: frontier recall (yes), <= half static growth (6.9% <= 13.9%, also under the tighter 10% prediction), rate <= 30% (25%), holdout calibration (out-of-fold throughout). Note: the shipped miss_threshold 0.668 escalates NOTHING (0%, recall stays 0.8958) - far-domain abstention and sufficiency escalation are different operating points on the same signal; the escalation gate needs its own calibrated threshold (~0.765 on this pile, H157 machinery applies)
- **Verdict** - **CONFIRMED** - a calibrated sufficiency gate on the free H181 top-seed signal recovers the full composed-frontier recall at a quarter of the static context cost; the REFUTED clause (no separating signal) is decisively off the table. The LLM self-report arm remains registered as a precision rider post-H363: it must beat 25% escalation rate / 4 surplus escalations to justify its latency, else the H181 threshold ships as the trigger
- **Engine wiring (2026-07-10)** - the gate shipped with the R38 tranche: `escalation_gate` lever (default off), `detect_escalation` beside `detect_miss`, two-tier threshold loader (R38-H384), rung-1 escalation render (proposition-seeded entities join `nodes` scored by their prop hit - the H367-B routing, escalation-scoped), `query.escalated`/`query.answered` events; full suite 411 passed. Rung 2 arrives free when the R34 span-render tranche (#70) lands; the gate then escalates through it unchanged

| Hypothesis | Persona | Lever | Grounding size | Pre-reg prediction | Acceptance bar |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R37-H382 | follower | calibrated sufficiency-gated escalation | need set = 8.3% of probes; static compose +27.8% | frontier recall at <= 10% mean growth, <= 30% escalation rate | frontier recall + <= half static growth + rate clause + holdout calibration |

Defects: DEF-13 (the ladder delivers the composed recovery at lower standing cost), DEF-14 (rung 0 is the parity harness - the ladder inherits the instrument fix). Sequencing: runs after the H363 ramp frees the local card (LLM self-report arm); the H181-threshold arm can run immediately on caches. Cross-round: R36-H380's outcome-linked worth could later price WHICH objects escalation renders first - noted, not claimed here.

## R38 - adaptive-gate self-calibration: ship a prior, calibrate per corpus, persist for future ingestions (registered 2026-07-10, user directive)

The user's directive, verbatim intent: "we can ship 'a priori' belief but we must be able to calibrate; and find a way to keep the calibration information for future ingestions (graph?)". H382 shipped the mechanism but not the constant: the fitted escalation cut (~0.765, out-of-fold 0.7650/0.7644) is pile-specific, and H157 (transfer ECE 0.2606 = 5.18x) forbids shipping it across corpus classes. Research-first per the standing rule - two briefs ground the round: the external literature brief ([`r38-calibration-literature-brief-20260710.md`](../../reports/r38-calibration-literature-brief-20260710.md); 11 papers archived with digests) and the internal map ([`r38-calibration-internal-map-20260710.md`](../../reports/r38-calibration-internal-map-20260710.md)). Three findings shape the hypotheses: (1) the fitted cut is EXACTLY the conformal-risk-control solution at alpha = 0.08 on the 24-outcome ledger ((24/25)(1/24) + 1/25 = 0.08) - the certificate machinery certifies what H382 already measured; (2) calibration-in-graph is shipped precedent, not greenfield - the resolver's isotonic curve persists in the KGFControl metanode today behind a file > graph > None precedence loader (pipeline.py:120-130, re-persisted at 454); (3) the missing piece is the outcome side - the engine records signal without outcome (`query.miss` carries top_score, no `query.answered` event exists), so labels today come only from offline probe replay. Units caveat pinned round-wide: every threshold is in Neo4j index-score units ((1 + cos)/2, not raw cosine - pipeline.py:789-790), the third operating point on one signal after miss 0.668 and abstention 0.75. Naive baseline: ship the pile-fitted 0.765 as a global constant - H157 predicts this fails on a new corpus class; every mechanism below must beat it there while not degrading the H382 result on this pile. Isotonic is contraindicated at n = 24 by both the literature (Platt beats isotonic below ~200-1000 calibration points, ICML 2005 learning curves - the H157 post-mortem citation) and our own H157 bill. The online-controller family (conformal PID / DtACI) is noted as a steady-state follow-on and deliberately NOT registered: its guarantees are asymptotic and vacuous at our label counts (ACI slack ~0.81 at T = 24) - it earns a hypothesis only when the harvested outcome stream reaches hundreds.

### R38-H383 CRC certificate - the fitted cut is a conformal risk control solution

- **Persona** - follower (the literature's strongest-fit mechanism validated against a result we already own)
- **Grounding** - Conformal Risk Control (arXiv 2208.02814, archived): lambda-hat = inf{lambda : (n/(n+1))R_n(lambda) + B/(n+1) <= alpha} gives E[loss] <= alpha exactly at finite n for monotone bounded losses; on the H382 ledger (n = 24, 1 residual miss at ~0.765) the alpha = 0.08 solution lands on the shipped operating point; feasibility floor alpha >= B/(n+1) = 0.04; infeasible alpha degrades to always-escalate, never to invalid
- **Hypothesis** - CRC over the H382 outcome ledger, with loss L_i(theta) = 1{probe i not fully covered under the gated policy at threshold theta}, reproduces the cross-validated cut and upgrades it from a fitted point to a certified one (E[miss] <= alpha, distribution-free, finite-sample)
- **Prediction** - theta_CRC(alpha = 0.08) within one candidate-cut step of 0.7650/0.7644; monotonicity premise holds on all 24 probes (rung-2 coverage is a superset of rung-0 coverage per probe - the composed frontier already recorded zero rung regressions); out-of-fold miss rate <= alpha
- **Acceptance bar** - CRC fit on the existing ledger reproduces the H382 threshold AND the monotone-loss premise verified probe-by-probe AND out-of-fold E[miss] <= alpha with the B/(n+1) slack reported; REFUTED if any probe answers at rung 0 but fails at rung 2 (monotonicity broken - fall back to split-conformal quantile on the miss indicator)
- **Experiment** - pure offline compute on the r37-h382-ladder per-probe records; no GPU, no LLM; extends the `fit_threshold` harness in `scripts/r37_h382_ladder.py` with the CRC objective
- **Result (2026-07-10)** - ([`r38-h383-crc-20260710T183423Z.json`](../../reports/r38-h383-crc-20260710T183423Z.json), `scripts/r38_h383_crc.py`) monotonicity premise HOLDS: zero rung-inversions on all 24 probes. **theta_CRC(alpha = 0.08) = 0.7644 - EXACTLY the fold-B fitted cut (delta 0.0)**, 0.00055 from fold A (well within one candidate-cut step); the CRC solution and the H382 empirical fit are the same operating point, as the brief's arithmetic predicted. Out-of-fold: pooled miss rate 0.0417 <= 0.08 (fold-A fit theta 0.76495 -> 1/12 miss on B, the unfixable P08; fold-B fit INFEASIBLE at n = 12 -> graceful always-escalate -> 0/12 on A). alpha sweep: 0.04 infeasible on this ledger (P08 parse loss pins min R_hat = 1/24, making 0.08 = (24/25)(1/24) + 1/25 the exact feasibility floor - the brief's coincidence is structural, not luck); 0.12 -> theta 0.7565 (escalates 5, accepts P22 as the second allowed miss). Honest note: the reader-class P08 sits inside the loss and charges the certificate - at fold size n = 12 it forces always-escalate, the conservative-safe direction; a parser fix (DEF class) would unlock alpha = 0.04
- **Verdict** - **CONFIRMED** - the fitted cut is a conformal risk control solution; the gate's threshold now carries a distribution-free finite-sample certificate (E[miss] <= 0.08) and, more importantly, a deterministic refit RULE any future corpus class can run on its own ledger. CRC ships as the fitting method in H384's persisted record (method = crc, alpha, n in provenance)

### R38-H384 Two-tier calibration state - prior in settings, certificate in the graph

- **Persona** - follower (copies the shipped resolver-calibration pattern verbatim, per the internal map)
- **Grounding** - internal map sections 1.3/4-5: the resolver curve persists in KGFControl today (pipeline.py:454) behind a file > graph > None loader (pipeline.py:120-130); `wipe()` is `MATCH (n) DETACH DELETE n` (pipeline.py:1250-1253) so graph state dies on rebuild and the settings prior is the wipe-survivor tier; the persisted curve is unversioned - the provenance precedent to copy is `data/processed/identity-calibration-v2.json` (version + full provenance block)
- **Hypothesis** - a `gate_calibration` record in the control metanode (threshold + provenance: hypothesis, fitted_at, corpus_fingerprint hashed over `processed_documents`, n_labels, oof_recall, escalation_rate, signal = top_seed_index_score) behind a `_make_gate_threshold` loader (file > graph > settings prior 0.765 flagged UNCERTIFIED) gives the gate the survival semantics the directive asks: survives incremental ingest/repair/repurpose, resets to the prior on wipe+rebuild, detects a mismatched pile by fingerprint
- **Prediction** - the survival matrix (internal map 5.4) reproduces under test: round-trip through ingest and repair unchanged, wipe -> prior, fingerprint mismatch flagged at load; no corruption from the documented stale-state read-modify-write hazard (internal map 4.3)
- **Acceptance bar** - integration tests green on all four survival-matrix rows plus the fingerprint-mismatch case AND the record carries the full provenance block; REFUTED if the metanode round-trip loses or corrupts the record under the stale-state hazard - then the record moves to its own node keyed by corpus fingerprint (also the multi-corpus-pile fix)
- **Experiment** - engine wiring + tests; no GPU; rides the H382 gate wiring (task #73) and touches settings.py (GraphRAGSettings: `escalation_gate`, `escalation_threshold_prior`, `escalation_min_labels`), the pipeline loader, and metanode round-trip tests
- **Result (wiring + tests, 2026-07-10)** - shipped with the H382 gate tranche: `escalation_gate` (default off) / `escalation_threshold_prior` 0.765 / `escalation_min_labels` 12 / `gate_calibration_path` in GraphRAGSettings; `_gate_threshold()` loader beside `_make_calibrator` with the registered precedence (file artifact > fingerprint-checked graph record > prior); record shape from `build_record()` carries the full provenance block (method=crc, alpha, n_labels, fitted_at, corpus_fingerprint). Tests green: loader precedence x3 + fingerprint-mismatch fallback + wipe-reset semantics (`tests/test_gate_calibration.py`), live metanode JSON round-trip of the record against the pile with prior-state restore (`tests/test_graph_integration.py::TestMetanode`, KGF_INTEGRATION=1). Survival rows 1-2 (incremental ingest / repair round-trip) inherit the shipped `calibration`-key mechanism (state loads whole, saves whole - pipeline.py ingest loop); verdict held until a live ingest cycles the record end-to-end

### R38-H385 Label harvesting - the engine mints its own calibration labels

- **Persona** - follower (Adaptive-RAG's outcome-labeled supervision - already the label type H382 measured)
- **Grounding** - literature brief section 3: replay labels (cheapest-rung-that-succeeded) trained Adaptive-RAG's entire complexity router without human annotation; internal map section 3: query answers are never persisted (no `query.answered` event; `query.miss` carries signal without outcome and is absent from the events.py SIGNALS registry; probe replay is the ONLY surface with the full query->outcome->signal triple, all offline); probe-mix caution carried from Adaptive-RAG: keep probe families balanced per class or the threshold calibrates to the probe mix, not the corpus
- **Hypothesis** - closing the outcome side (a `query.answered` event carrying question + top_score + answer path, plus a `fit_gate_from_events` twin of `fit_calibration_from_events` joining events to probe gold) makes gate calibration self-serve: a probe replay through the ENGINE harvests the same labels the offline script computes, and `optimize()` refits the threshold above the min-labels floor
- **Prediction** - engine-harvested labels on the 24-probe replay reproduce the offline ledger (same 24 signal values, same outcomes); the fit from harvested labels lands within one candidate-cut step of the offline fit; the refit hook fires at `optimize()` only at n >= `escalation_min_labels`
- **Acceptance bar** - label-for-label match between engine-harvested and offline ledgers AND fit equivalence AND floor discipline (no refit below the floor); REFUTED if the engine path cannot reproduce the offline labels (instrumentation changes the measurement) - then labels stay an offline artifact and the graph stores only the fitted record
- **Experiment** - engine wiring (events.py SIGNALS registration including the currently unregistered `query.miss`, pipeline.py query/optimize hooks, new fitter beside `detect_miss`) + a replay run; LLM calls ride the local engine post-H363
- **Result (wiring + fitter equivalence, 2026-07-10)** - outcome side closed: `query.answered` emitted from `query()` carrying question + `seed_top_score` (pure gate signal, distinct from the composite abstention score) + `escalated` + answer + path; `query.miss` and `query.escalated` registered in events.py SIGNALS (registry drift fixed). `fit_gate_from_events()` in the new `graph/gate_calibration.py` joins two-pass replay events (escalated false = rung-0 arm, true = rung-2 arm) against probe gold with the CRC fitter; fit equivalence VERIFIED on the recorded ledger - the ported fitter reproduces the H383 certificate exactly (theta(0.08) = 0.7644, 0.04 infeasible, 0.12 = 0.7565 - pinned as unit tests); floor discipline and incomplete-pair skipping tested. Deferred with reasons: the engine-replay label-for-label match clause waits on rung 2 in the engine (R34 tranche #70) and LLM availability (post-H363); the `optimize()` refit hook lands with that replay stage - without an in-engine label source (events path + gold) it would have nothing to consume, and inventing a store now would outrun the registration
- **Result (engine replay, answer side + refit hook, 2026-07-11)** - ([`r38-h385-answer-replay-20260711T103338Z.json`](../../reports/r38-h385-answer-replay-20260711T103338Z.json), `scripts/r38_h385_answer_replay.py`) two full-query passes (rung-0 gate-off / rung-2 always-escalate) through the shipped `query()` with LLM answers on the local engine. The label-match clause **REFUTED on its registered branch**: `_covered` grades verbatim gold-substring-in-ANSWER, and the LLM paraphrases - 17 of 19 paired probes read uncovered at BOTH rungs (offline context-side: 3 at rung 0, 1 at rung 2), so the CRC fit went infeasible (theta 0.8851, escalate-everything) vs the offline 0.7644; 5 of 24 probes never paired (abstention emits `query.miss`, not a second `query.answered`); signals drifted <= 0.0335 vs the H382 ladder (pile evolution). Instrumentation changes the measurement - engine-minted ANSWER labels are not the offline CONTEXT labels; per registration, labels stay an offline artifact and the graph stores only the fitted record. The `optimize()` refit hook itself SHIPPED: `Foundry._refit_gate()` (pipeline.py) harvests `fit_gate_from_events` against `graphrag.gate_probe_set` under the `escalation_min_labels` floor, persists the record to control state with the corpus fingerprint; floor discipline (no write below floor), persist path, and gate/probe-set guards unit-tested (440 tests green). Sink gotcha for future scripts: the event log is a module global wired by the CLI - scripts must call `enable_event_log` themselves
- **Result (engine replay, context side, 2026-07-10 late)** - ([`r38-h385-engine-replay-20260710T200750Z.json`](../../reports/r38-h385-engine-replay-20260710T200750Z.json), `scripts/r38_h385_engine_replay.py`) with rung 2 landed, all 24 probes replayed through the SHIPPED `_retrieve_local` on the benchmark pile in three arms, retrieval-only. Passages built live through the engine's own machinery first (4,253 KGFPassage nodes, bge-m3 on GPU 2 via the passage channel). The gated arm at the shipped prior 0.765 (the H384 cold-start path - the pile carries no `gate_calibration` record) escalated EXACTLY the offline pooled-holdout set [P01, P06, P08, P12, P15, P22] - the engine's live gate signal reproduces the offline instrument's decisions label-for-label on the escalation side. Recall: gate_off 0.7917 (16/24 full), gated 0.8125 (17/24 full, +5.0% context), always-escalate 0.8125 (17/24 full, +20.4% context) - the gate captures 100% of the always-escalate recall gain at a quarter of its context cost, zero regressions in either escalating arm; the single mover is P12 (0.5 -> 1.0 via the appended span), and the 18 surplus always-escalations buy nothing (consistent with offline: escalation pays only where the miss is span-recoverable). Caveat: the pile has no `kgf_proposition_embeddings` index, so rung 1 never fired (proposition retrieval unavailable, logged and gracefully skipped) - the additive-vs-displacement rung-1 parity question is NOT adjudicated by this run; the measured gains are pure gate + rung 2. Still open on H385: the LLM answer side of the label match (post-H363) and the `optimize()` refit hook

### R38-H386 Drift-triggered refit - the certificate knows when it expired

- **Persona** - follower (the brief's cheapest first experiment: S4 is a strict subset of the full S1 design)
- **Grounding** - Podkopaev-Ramdas (arXiv 2110.06177, archived): sequential harmful-shift test with P(false alarm EVER) <= delta valid under continuous monitoring - fixed-n tests re-run continuously provably inflate false alarms; t-digest / KLL sketches (both archived): mergeable label-free summaries of the gate-signal distribution, and the threshold sits at ~the 25th percentile of class scores, not a tail, so both sketches are adequate; harmful-vs-benign distinction is the point - a moved score distribution with held recall must NOT trigger refit
- **Hypothesis** - a per-class monitor (confidence-sequence running miss rate on harvested outcomes + a mergeable score sketch over all gate evaluations) detects harmful drift within the label budget of two ingestions and stays silent on benign re-ingestion, making refit event-driven instead of per-ingestion
- **Prediction** - injected harmful shift (swap the signal embedder, or run a different corpus register against the stored class state) alarms within <= 2 replay batches; benign re-ingestion of the same corpus never alarms; the sketch quantile shift at theta flags the embedder swap even before any outcome label lands
- **Acceptance bar** - both injected-harm cases alarm within budget AND zero false alarms across >= 3 benign re-ingestions AND the sketch flags the label-free case; REFUTED if the monitor is underpowered at KGF label rates (tens of outcomes per replay) - then refit falls back to per-ingestion recompute from the merged ledger (cheap: O(n log n))
- **Experiment** - offline: replay ledgers + synthetic shift injection; sketch built from the unlabeled signal stream of the replay runs; no new GPU load
- **Result (2026-07-10)** - ([`r38-h386-monitor-20260710T185118Z.json`](../../reports/r38-h386-monitor-20260710T185118Z.json), `scripts/r38_h386_monitor.py`) two-channel monitor over 500-seed bootstrap replays of the H382 ledger at the certified cut (outcome channel: mixture-LR supermartingale, H0 miss <= alpha + 0.02 = 0.10, delta 0.05, state mergeable by addition; signal channel: escalation-rate-at-theta vs stored class rate, 3-sigma band). Guarantee side PERFECT: benign ever-alarm 0.000 (budget 0.05), the registered 3-ingestion benign run silent on both channels. Detection side UNDERPOWERED at 24-label batches: harm A (embedder swap -> gate dead, miss 0.125) outcome-alarms in only 10.8% of runs (6.8% within the 2-batch budget) - a 2.5-point breach over tolerance needs O(1/gap^2) ~ hundreds of labels, information-theoretic, not fixable by a better test; harm B (register change, miss ~0.29) alarms 65.6% by batch 5 but only 29.8% within 2. The sketch channel carries harm A instead: 97.2% detection (median batch 3 at replay volumes; production query volumes are 10-100x per ingestion, making it near-immediate) - the label-free channel detects exactly the failure the label channel cannot afford
- **Verdict** - **REFUTED** (the registered within-2-batch outcome-alarm clause, at KGF label rates - the anticipated failure mode) - fallback adopted per registration: the primary refresh is per-ingestion CRC recompute from the merged ledger (O(n log n), and it is the H383 rule anyway); the sequential monitor is retained as the zero-false-alarm tripwire between replays (it never cries wolf - benign 0.000 vs delta 0.05), and the label-free sketch channel is promoted to first detector for signal-scale breaches (embedder swap class). Two-channel design validated in shape, not in the registered latency

### R38-H387 Cross-class transfer done right - prior warm-start earns a per-class certificate

- **Persona** - contrarian (the H157 transfer test re-run with machinery built to pass it; the dominance clause can kill the belief layer)
- **Grounding** - H157: transferred fitted constants = ECE 0.2606 = 5.18x; literature brief exchangeability discipline: ship the METHOD and the PRIOR globally, scope calibration SETS and CERTIFICATES per corpus class; hierarchical Beta-Binomial pseudo-counts (posterior-sampling machinery per the archived Thompson Sampling tutorial) are the literal "a-priori belief that calibrates" - works from n = 0, merges by count addition - but carry model-based credibility only, never a distribution-free guarantee
- **Hypothesis** - on a NEW corpus class, the shipped prior (0.765 flagged UNCERTIFIED) plus per-class label accumulation earns a certified per-class threshold whose measured miss respects alpha; the naive shipped constant either lands outside the class's certified band (H157 repeats - prior alone insufficient) or inside it (the constant was accidentally portable - and the certificate PROVES it rather than assumes it)
- **Prediction** - the class's certified threshold differs from 0.765 by more than one candidate-cut step (H157 says constants do not transfer); yet the prior-started gate never underperforms never-escalate while UNCERTIFIED (over-escalation costs context, not recall - the safe failure direction); the Beta-Binomial warm-start reaches the certified cut with fewer labels than a cold start
- **Acceptance bar** - certified per-class threshold with out-of-fold miss <= alpha on the new class AND zero recall regression vs never-escalate during the UNCERTIFIED phase; belief-layer dominance clause: if the posterior credible interval at n = 24 is wider than the conformal certificate is tight, the Beta-Binomial layer is strictly dominated - drop it and ship prior-as-constant-until-certified; REFUTED (for the round's premise) if the certified threshold's measured miss exceeds alpha beyond the B/(n+1) slack - probe non-exchangeability WITHIN a class
- **Experiment** - gated on a second corpus class with a probe set (the #59 public-benchmark build is the natural host); everything upstream (H383-H386) is class-independent and runs first

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R38-H383 | follower | CRC certificate on the H382 ledger | n = 24 outcomes; alpha floor 0.04 | theta_CRC(0.08) = fitted cut; zero rung-inversions | cut match + monotonicity + oof miss <= alpha |
| R38-H384 | follower | two-tier state (settings prior + graph record) | shipped precedent pipeline.py:120-130/454 | survival matrix reproduces | 4 matrix rows + fingerprint + provenance block |
| R38-H385 | follower | query.answered + fit_gate_from_events | 24-probe replay; Adaptive-RAG precedent | engine labels = offline labels | label match + fit equivalence + floor discipline |

| R38-H387 | contrarian | prior warm-start -> per-class certificate | H157 ECE 5.18x; new class n >= 24 | class cut != 0.765; no recall loss while UNCERTIFIED | certified oof miss <= alpha + no-regression + dominance clause |

Sequencing: H383 immediately (pure offline on existing reports); H384/H385 ride the H382 engine-wiring tranche (task #73, with the R34 tranche #70); H386 offline once H385's ledger format exists; H387 gated on the second corpus class (#59). ## R39 - graph-construction round: dense + sparse + passages, and the graph that proves it missed nothing (user-directed, registered 2026-07-11)

User strategic direction (2026-07-11, verbatim intent): build the SOTA graph FIRST - trustworthy, does not miss information, with dense and sparse node classes and specific passages per HippoRAG's published science; the retriever bridge between the querying AI and the graph comes after, on top of that substrate. Research-first per the standing rule: the round is grounded by [`r39-graph-construction-literature-brief-20260711.md`](../../reports/r39-graph-construction-literature-brief-20260711.md) (HippoRAG 1/2 construction anatomy with exact numbers; 6 new papers archived with digests: SiReRAG, KGGen, PropRAG, EDC, GraphJudge, ODKE+). Load-bearing open slot: KGGen's MINE protocol proves every published extractor retains only 29.8-66.1% of source facts, yet NO system - HippoRAG included - measures its own per-document fact coverage at ingest; a coverage CERTIFICATE instead of a hope is unclaimed territory and is the user's "does not miss information" mandate made falsifiable. Fence: R34 owns the transferred retrieval levers (H366/H367 CONFIRMED, H369/H370 REFUTED, H368 gated on the benchmark harness); R35 owns questions, R36 maintenance, R37/R38 escalation + calibration. Naive baseline: the post-R34 live graph + composed frontier (0.9583 / 23-of-24); coverage hypotheses define instrument-first baselines. Full mechanism, grounding citations and bar details per hypothesis live in the brief - the registrations below pin persona, prediction and acceptance bar.

### R39-H389 Extraction-coverage certificate - the graph measures what it missed (conformist)

- **Mechanism/Grounding** - per-doc ingest audit: verbatim-grounded fact probes sampled per chunk, graph-support test (entity/prop/span + entailment), per-doc coverage score + miss list into the gap ledger; KGGen MINE (15 facts/article, retention 29.8-66.1%), ODKE+ Grounder pattern
- **Prediction** - KGF per-chunk fact coverage on the 10-doc corpus measures BELOW 90%; the P08 '9.0W' parse loss surfaces in the miss list blind
- **Acceptance bar** - audit rediscovers >= 2 known failure classes untold; coverage reproducible +-2% across re-runs; audit cost <= 25% of ingest cost; fence: if R35-H373's answerability ledger already surfaces the same misses, record subsumption honestly

### R39-H390 Multi-pass extraction union - the coverage-vs-passes curve (conformist)

- **Mechanism/Grounding** - k independent extraction passes per chunk (temperature/prompt-perm), triples unioned through the Bayesian resolver, marginal unique-fact yield measured with H389; KGGen ceiling, H107 variance 71%, EDC+R iteration
- **Prediction** - pass 2 adds >= 8% new true facts; marginal yield decays geometrically (pass 3 < half of pass 2)
- **Acceptance bar** - coverage gain per dollar beats single-pass-with-larger-model head-to-head AND identity metrics do not regress; REFUTED if pass-2 yield < 3% (single pass near ceiling)

### R39-H391 Passages become graph citizens - entity-to-span context edges (conformist)

- **Mechanism/Grounding** - materialize HippoRAG-2's "contains" linkage: direct edges between (:KGFPassage) spans and entities extracted from their carrier chunk (today spans are pool-only, 2-hop via the Chunk hub); HippoRAG 2 Table 4 (passage nodes -6.1 R@5 when removed), reset weights 1.0/0.05
- **Prediction** - inert on the CPAP 1-hop render (H37 containment) but REQUIRED substrate for H368: at benchmark scale, PPR over the unified entity+span graph beats entity-only PPR on passage recall
- **Acceptance bar** - edge build idempotent, zero live-harness regressions, storage priced; the recall claim rides H368's registered gate (+3 R@5 at <= 1.5x latency); REFUTED as construction if H368 promotes equally without context edges

### R39-H392 Open-extraction shadow pass - measure what the 26-type ontology drops (conformist)

- **Mechanism/Grounding** - schema-free OpenIE pass on a doc sample beside the production constrained pass, fact-set diff through H389's support test; EDC open-extract-then-canonicalize, HippoRAG schema-free layer
- **Prediction** - open pass yields >= 10% facts with no typed-graph support, concentrated in relation vocabulary outside the cured ontology
- **Acceptance bar** - pre-registered decision rule: leakage >= 10% -> EDC-style canonicalize stage enters the R40 design; < 3% -> cure-then-constrain vindicated; between -> classify and re-fan; REFUTED only if the diff instrument disagrees with a hand-checked sample (< 80%)

### R39-H393 The dense/sparse duality does NOT transfer to a typed-merged graph (contrarian)

- **Mechanism/Grounding** - claim: HippoRAG's synonym-edge mass (7-9x relation edges, tau=0.8) and context substrate COMPENSATE for absent entity resolution and typing; KGF's Bayesian merges already collapse those clusters; H1 Table 5 (+2.4 avg from synonymy without ER), R34-H370 flat SIMILAR_TO traversal
- **Prediction** - benchmark-scale A/B (merged-typed graph vs same + synonym edges at 0.8): delta < 1 point passage R@5; HippoRAG's own ablation ordering inverts under merging
- **Acceptance bar** - on the H368 harness: CONFIRMED if delta < 1 point (synonym layer retired, budget freed); REFUTED if >= 2 points (alias layer earns first-class status, built to H370's creator-gap spec)

### R39-H394 Coverage-gated ingest - the document is not ingested until the graph proves it missed nothing (heretical)

- **Mechanism/Grounding** - close the loop nobody closes: H389's miss list drives TARGETED re-extraction (prompt carries the miss + its source span), iterate extract -> audit -> re-extract until coverage >= threshold or marginal yield < epsilon; terminal misses CERTIFIED in the ledger, never silent; ODKE+ gap-driven extraction (19M facts, 98.8% precision) targets only cross-corpus staleness, never intra-document recall
- **Prediction** - 2-3 targeted iterations lift per-doc coverage to >= 97% at <= 1.8x single-pass cost; targeted passes recover misses blind union (H390) cannot; P08-class losses recovered or certified-unrecoverable at parser level
- **Acceptance bar** - coverage lift per unit cost beats H390 at equal spend; zero fidelity regressions on a judged sample; certified-miss list human-verified once (>= 80% real source facts); GATED on H389 confirming its instrument

### R39-H395 Verified admission with a rejection ledger - precision that cannot silently cost coverage (follower)

- **Mechanism/Grounding** - per-triple admission check against the source span before graph commit, rejections QUARANTINED as ledger objects (provenance-carrying, auditable), never dropped; GraphJudge (7B judge > 90% triple accuracy), ODKE+ Grounder (98.8% ingest precision) - neither accounts for false rejections
- **Prediction** - on seeded synthetic corruptions the gate catches >= 80% while rejecting <= 2% of true triples; rejected-true triples map to known fidelity classes (model_code SAME_AS surface -> the quarantine becomes the H101 adjudication queue)
- **Acceptance bar** - corruption-catch and true-rejection rates on a labeled sample; end-to-end recall unchanged (quarantine, never drop); per-triple cost <= 15% of extraction (small local judge, idle-card economics); REFUTED if judge accuracy on the technical corpus < 75%

| Hypothesis | Persona | Lever | Pre-reg prediction | Acceptance bar |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| R39-H389 | conformist | per-doc extraction-coverage audit + certificate | coverage < 90%, P08 rediscovered blind | 2 known classes rediscovered, +-2% reproducible, <= 25% ingest cost |
| R39-H390 | conformist | multi-pass extraction union | pass 2 >= +8% true facts, geometric decay | beats larger-model spend, identity flat; REFUTED < 3% |
| R39-H391 | conformist | entity->span context edges | substrate required for H368 at scale | idempotent, zero regressions; adjudicated by H368's gate |
| R39-H392 | conformist | open-extraction shadow diff | >= 10% leakage outside cured ontology | decision rule 10%/3% pre-registered |
| R39-H393 | contrarian | synonym-edge layer under merging | delta < 1 point R@5 (duality = ER compensation) | < 1 CONFIRMED-retire; >= 2 REFUTED-build |
| R39-H394 | heretical | coverage-gated ingest loop + certificate | >= 97% coverage in 2-3 targeted iterations at <= 1.8x cost | beats blind union per dollar; certified misses verified |
| R39-H395 | follower | verified admission + rejection quarantine | >= 80% corruption catch, <= 2% true rejection | recall unchanged, <= 15% cost, judge >= 75% on-domain |

Sequencing: H389 first (it is the instrument every other coverage hypothesis consumes), H392 rides its instrument, H390 then H394 (gated on H389), H395 parallel (independent labeled-sample work); H391/H393 ride the #59 benchmark harness (H368's gate). Execution shares the GPU economy with the benchmark campaign - coverage audits are idle-card work per the H363 pattern.

## R40 - retrieval bridge round: the best retrieval in one go, hops priced and rare (user-directed, registered 2026-07-11)

User direction (2026-07-11): the bridge between the querying AI and the graph is governed by QUERY TOKEN ECONOMICS - best retrieval in ONE round trip; multiple hops are a rare, calibrated, priced exception. Grounded by [`r40-retrieval-bridge-literature-brief-20260711.md`](../../reports/r40-retrieval-bridge-literature-brief-20260711.md) (5 papers archived: ToG, Graph-CoT, IRCoT, CRAG, Plan-on-Graph). Decisive published number: HippoRAG single-shot multi-hop is 10-30x cheaper and 6-13x faster than IRCoT at comparable-or-better recall (H1 Appendix G Table 17); the optimized agentic walk still bills 8,156 tok / 13.3 LLM calls per question (PoG Table 4) where KGF's pipe spends 2 calls. Fence: R37/R38 own the escalation gate + its calibration; R34-H368 owns gated PPR; R39 owns construction. Every bar carries a tokens-per-answered-query clause. EXECUTION GATED on the R39 substrate + #59 benchmark harness; registration now so the round starts the moment the graph is ready.

### R40-H396 One-response bridge contract (follower) - answer + evidence + provenance + calibrated confidence + coverage verdict + cost, in a single round trip; bar: quality flat vs the current pipe, total bill <= current +5%
### R40-H397 Adversary arm (conformist) - bounded ToG/PoG-style walk implemented on KGF's own graph, head-to-head vs single-shot+escalation on recall-per-1k-tokens; closes the never-published cost-adjusted comparison; bar: verdict either way with paired-design significance
### R40-H398 The single-shot ceiling (contrarian) - one reformulated second retrieval round (no walk) is irreducible on the compositional class; prediction: fires <= 15% of queries and becomes ladder rung 3; refutation = single-shot doctrine confirmed even there
### R40-H399 Certified-negative retrieval (conformist, proven-novel) - bridge consults R39 coverage certificates/miss ledger and answers "not in corpus - certified" in ZERO extra rounds; web-verified unpublished (CRAG/SABER-class infer insufficiency from query-time signals only); GATED on R39-H389; bar: certified negatives at 100% precision on seeded-absence probes, zero token overhead on positives
### R40-H400 Upfront decomposition without the loop (follower) - PoG-style sub-objective decomposition as ONE cheap call, batched sub-retrievals, one answer call; captures the walk's guidance (+3.1 in PoG's ablation) at fixed 3-call cost; bar: recall gain on multi-hop class at <= 1.5x single-shot bill
### R40-H401 Negotiation surface (follower) - calibrated gate metadata exposed to the caller so escalation is BOUGHT, not automatic; bar: >= 90% agreement with the internal gate, metadata <= 5% of response tokens
### R40-H402 Zero graph work at query time (heretical) - renders precompiled at optimize() as versioned artifacts; query = embed + fetch + answer; R36 owns invalidation; bar: quality flat, query-time latency and tokens strictly dominated

## R41 - speculative graph construction round: ingestion-time entity pinning + the provisional tier (user-directed, registered 2026-07-11)

User concept (2026-07-11, course-corrected): the PRIMARY sense is INGESTION-TIME - while extracting a chunk, a speculative in-memory fragment spanning the committed graph and the in-flight document (candidate entities, aliases, distinguishing facts) expands the extraction context so the LLM PINS mentions to existing identities instead of re-inventing them; judged on PIN PRECISION, never just naming consistency. Targets H107's 71% extraction variance upstream (H119 canonicalized WORDING and was REFUTED - it never fed the GRAPH back into extraction). Grounded by [`r41-speculative-graph-literature-brief-20260711.md`](../../reports/r41-speculative-graph-literature-brief-20260711.md) (10 papers archived: ReLiK, GENRE, KB-Injection, iText2KG, NELL, Knowledge Vault, Speculative Decoding, Speculative RAG, DrKIT, UKGE). Load-bearing slot: candidate injection is proven against STATIC curated KBs (ReLiK/GENRE); nobody has measured graph -> context -> extraction -> graph identity quality against a GROWING self-extracted graph; iText2KG's measured -10% anchoring cost is the standing warning. Trust contract: speculation never enters the committed tier unverified. Fence: R39 owns coverage/extraction-recall levers; the resolver stack (H106/H129) is the downstream repair this round tries to pre-empt.

### R41-H403 Spanning-context injection A/B (conformist) - candidate entities + distinguishing facts injected into the extraction prompt; prediction: cross-doc duplicate creation -30%, pin precision >= 95%, H107 variance measurably down; bar: identity fragmentation and pin precision vs the un-injected baseline on the same doc stream, paired design
### R41-H404 Candidate retrieval policy (conformist) - what to inject: kNN entities vs typed neighborhood vs recent-document working set vs facts-vs-names-only; bar: winning policy beats naive kNN by >= 5 points pin precision at equal context cost
### R41-H405 Pin economics (follower) - acceptance-rate ledger prices injection cost vs resolver-repair savings; prediction: net ingest cost goes NEGATIVE past ~10 documents; bar: measured net token delta on a full corpus ingest
### R41-H406 Pin ledger (follower) - every pin/decline persisted as an auditable identity decision with provenance; bar: >= 80% recovery on seeded wrong-pin corruptions; composes with H389 (a pin is auditable; "fact extracted, wrong pin" becomes a new miss class)
### R41-H407 Anchoring risk (contrarian) - decoy-seeded injection: the LLM pins to plausible-but-wrong candidates; prediction: pin precision < 90% under decoys and wrong pins cost MORE than downstream repair (iText2KG -10% precedent); a confirmation caps injection at high-confidence candidates only
### R41-H408 Structural bridge candidates (follower, secondary sense) - link-prediction-style bridge-candidate generator feeding a NELL/Knowledge-Vault-style two-tier provisional ledger; fixes H370's creator gap (the needed bridges never existed); bar: bridge candidates verified before promotion, walk recall gain at zero committed-tier contamination
### R41-H409 Trust as a predicate (heretical) - invert the tiers: NOTHING is committed; trust is a per-object predicate earned via audits (H389/H395 machinery), the untrusted residue becomes a failure detector; prediction: residue is >= 3x enriched in real defects; bar: enrichment measured on adjudicated defect labels

Sequencing: R41-H403 is the round's instrument-and-lever in one (needs the H101-class pin labels); H404/H405 ride its harness; H407 runs adversarially beside H403 (same runs, decoy arm); H406 wires after H403 confirms; H408/H409 are post-benchmark. R42 (metrics framework) brief is in flight - its candidates number from H410.

## R42 - metrics framework round: genuinely measurable, gradient-trackable (user-directed, registered 2026-07-11)

User directive (2026-07-11): metrics that make quality genuinely measurable and let us track the gradient of quality with respect to hyperparameters and hypotheses. Grounded by [`r42-metrics-framework-literature-brief-20260711.md`](../../reports/r42-metrics-framework-literature-brief-20260711.md) (6 papers archived: Miller error-bars-for-evals, Urbano IR significance, RAGAS, ARES, Prediction-Powered Inference, MT-Bench judge reliability). Load-bearing power finding: Miller's paired formula gives n ~= 969 questions to detect a 3-point delta at 80% power (alpha 0.05) - the #59 1000-question sets natively resolve ~3 points (MDE 2.95); a 1-point delta needs ~8,700 questions or variance reduction; naive SEs on document-clustered questions run up to 3x too small; the 24-probe harness resolves nothing below ~6 points at N=3. Fence: H351/DEF-11 own the lesson this round generalizes; H172 owns the entailment instrument; R39-H389 supplies the deterministic coverage metric. Gradient = finite-difference sensitivity on measured response curves.

### R42-H410 Metric registry with measured noise floors (conformist) - every shipped metric gets N>=3 replay sigma; hypothesis bars auto-priced off the registry (H351 generalized); prediction: >= 3 shipped metrics have floors ABOVE deltas past rounds ruled on
### R42-H411 Paired per-question A/Bs with clustered SEs (conformist) - paired Welch on the #59 sets, SEs clustered by source document; prediction: pairing cuts SE >= 30%, 2-point deltas become decidable at n=1000
### R42-H412 PPI-rectified judge metrics (follower) - local judge scores ALL items, 150-300 adjudicated labels rectify the bias (prediction-powered inference); prediction: CIs >= 30% narrower at equal label budget vs labels-only
### R42-H413 Staged attribution chain (conformist) - extraction coverage -> resolution -> seed -> render -> answer, each stage a conditional metric; prediction: >= 80% of past confirmed levers attribute to exactly one stage blind
### R42-H414 Judge noise floor (contrarian) - LLM-judge replay sigma >= 2 points vs <= 0.5 for exact-match: judge metrics CANNOT track our gradients; only deterministic instruments gate, judges demote to supporting evidence
### R42-H415 Retire the 24-probe harness as a gate (heretical) - prediction: it cannot separate adjacent post-R34 configs at N=5 replays while the paired #59 form separates all; it survives only as a smoke test
### R42-H416 Variance IS the objective (heretical) - an extraction-form lever cutting run sigma 0.0290 -> <= 0.0175 at flat recall outranks any recall lever (n scales with sigma^2 - halves every future MDE); prediction: at least one such lever exists in the R41 pinning family
### R42-H417 MDE-stepped response curves (follower) - finite-difference sweeps per knob with steps sized to the metric's MDE; prediction: >= 2 smooth tunable knobs (render budget, top_k) + >= 1 correctly-detected cliff (concurrency capacity)

Minimum viable suite (ships first, pure replay, no ingests): the metric registry with measured sigmas (H410), paired-delta + clustered-SE verdict form on the #59 sets (H411), deterministic chain as primary gates (H389 coverage, fragmentation census, EM recall, tokens-per-query) with judge metrics demoted until H414 prices them. Sequencing: H410/H411 immediately post-benchmark-harness; H414 prices judges before any judge-gated verdict; H413 rides the first full benchmark ingest; H415/H416/H417 follow the registry.

## R43 - graph-structure forensics round: measure the shift, route the blame (user-directed, registered 2026-07-11)

User directive (2026-07-11): research the graph structure with graph-theory metrics and their CHANGES over ingestion, correlated with the sharp drop when gold retrieval stopped (REG-1: passed <= 135 docs, failed from 154 - `results/bench/regression-ledger.md`), to catch anomalous ingestion behavior or a structural phase shift; findings inform the retriever side; wide fanout. PRE-REGISTRATION DATUM already banked: the R43 time-series instrument (`scripts/r43_structural_timeseries.py`) ran on the 226-doc pile - GLOBAL structure is smooth through the REG-1 window (giant share 0.32->0.31->0.33, max degree flat 34, hub share declining; gelation jumps at docs 82/119 precede the failure harmlessly) - the contrarian family enters with evidence leaning its way. Fence: R09 owns static topology (H61-H80), H47 the Heaps marker, R36 drift/maintenance, R42 the statistical verdict machinery every alarm threshold must use (bootstrap-priced, never magic). **Family A - structural time-series instrumentation (offline-cheap)**: H418 created_at filtration instrument dump-validated at boundaries; H419 densification-slope breakpoint (Leskovec law segmented regression); H420 per-label-class Heaps break with shuffle-null; H421 component series + gold-component membership tracking; H422 DeltaCon step series with node attribution; H423 hub-condensation order parameter k_max/E

Verdict discipline: no structural alarm CONFIRMS without clearing its bootstrap band (R42) and beating the H443 doc-counter null.

- **Result (H429+H430 router on REG-1, 2026-07-11, same day)** - ([`h429-h430-router-20260711T155622Z.jsonl`](../../results/r43/h429-h430-router-20260711T155622Z.jsonl), `scripts/r43_h429_h430_router.py`; rank-at-time-t reconstructed offline by restricting the fixed question-to-entity cosine ranking to entities born <= t) - **THE GRAPH IS INNOCENT; the round routes RETRIEVER-SIDE**. H429: both gold film->director paths connected at EVERY t in [103, 200] - no break anywhere near the (135, 154] transition.
